[]{#vocab.terralid.org#c_cad56412}

##  site type
- **Child of**:
 [`Konzepte`](#000024)
- Set of categories to characterise the use of a site by humans or its
geological relevance.
- **Concept URI:** http://vocab.terralid.org#c_cad56412
- **Other Properties:**

[]{#vocab.terralid.org#c_241d96ca}

###  anthropogenic site
- **Child of**:
 [`site type`](#vocab.terralid.org#c_cad56412)
- The site is characterised by traces of human activity without any
further specification.
- **Concept URI:** http://vocab.terralid.org#c_241d96ca
- **Other Properties:**

[]{#vocab.terralid.org#c_4327d421}

- **Child of**:
 [`environnement bâti`](#000018)

- **Other Properties:**

[]{#vocab.terralid.org#c_a4821cf4}

#####  camp
- **Child of**:
 [`built structures`](#vocab.terralid.org#c_4327d421)

architecture.

- **Other Properties:**

[]{#vocab.terralid.org#c_c8fd0d05}

city, 
town,

- **Child of**:
 [`anthropogenic site`](#vocab.terralid.org#c_241d96ca)
- Material used to fill in a negative feature in the ground.
- **Concept URI:** http://vocab.terralid.org#c_536290b6
- **Other Properties:**

[]{#vocab.terralid.org#c_8f51e8b9}

####  storage pit
- **Child of**:
 [`anthropogenic site`](#vocab.terralid.org#c_241d96ca)
- A pit dug in the ground to store materials.
- **Concept URI:** http://vocab.terralid.org#c_8f51e8b9
- **Other Properties:**

[]{#vocab.terralid.org#c_823bba89}

###  geological site
- **Child of**:
 [`υλικά μορφώματα`](#000019)
 [`site type`](#vocab.terralid.org#c_cad56412)
- The site is characterised by its geological features.
- **Concept URI:** http://vocab.terralid.org#c_823bba89
- **Other Properties:**

[]{#vocab.terralid.org#c_1f2bd128}

####  cave
- **Child of**:
 [`geological site`](#vocab.terralid.org#c_823bba89)
- A natural opening in the earth large enough for humans to explore
and sometimes also to use.
- **Concept URI:** http://vocab.terralid.org#c_1f2bd128
- **Other Properties:**

[]{#vocab.terralid.org#c_00c534fa}

#####  burial cave
- **Child of**:
 [`funerary site`](#vocab.terralid.org#c_87b21259)
 [`cave`](#vocab.terralid.org#c_1f2bd128)
- A natural opening in the earth used to bury deceased humans,
sometimes animals.
- **Concept URI:** http://vocab.terralid.org#c_00c534fa
- **Other Properties:**

[]{#vocab.terralid.org#c_4d90feaa}

####  deposit
- **Child of**:
 [`geological site`](#vocab.terralid.org#c_823bba89)
- A single body of naturally occurring and genetically connected
material with an anomalous concentration of a mineral or rock type
that has potential for human utilisation.
- **Concept URI:** http://vocab.terralid.org#c_4d90feaa
- **Other Properties:**

[]{#vocab.terralid.org#c_177cd232}

####  occurrence
- **Child of**:
 [`geological site`](#vocab.terralid.org#c_823bba89)
- A single body of naturally occurring and genetically connected
material of any concentration that might have potential for human
utilisation.
- **Alternate labels:**
mineralisation
- **Concept URI:** http://vocab.terralid.org#c_177cd232
- **Other Properties:**

[]{#vocab.terralid.org#c_9db8b2d9}

#####  outcrop
- **Child of**:
 [`occurrence`](#vocab.terralid.org#c_177cd232)
- An occurrence that is accessible on the ground.
- **Concept URI:** http://vocab.terralid.org#c_9db8b2d9
- **Other Properties:**

[]{#vocab.terralid.org#c_4fba9c43}

####  field
- **Child of**:
 [`geological site`](#vocab.terralid.org#c_823bba89)
- An area characterised by several geologically related mineral
occurrences.
- **Concept URI:** http://vocab.terralid.org#c_4fba9c43
- **Other Properties:**

[]{#vocab.terralid.org#c_b01341cd}

####  locality
- **Child of**:
 [`geological site`](#vocab.terralid.org#c_823bba89)
- An area characterised by several geologically related mineral
occurrences with potential of human utilisation.
- **Alternate labels:**
mining prospect
- **Concept URI:** http://vocab.terralid.org#c_b01341cd
- **Other Properties:**

##  assemblage
- **Child of**:
 [`Konzepte`](#000024)
- Grouping of items or specimens resulting from human activity for
archaeological materials or environmental and tectonic processes for
geological materials.
- **Concept URI:** http://vocab.terralid.org#c_0fab0ef9
- **Other Properties:**

[]{#vocab.terralid.org#c_d0a53b23}

###  collection
- **Child of**:
 [`Konzepte`](#000024)
 [`assemblage`](#vocab.terralid.org#c_0fab0ef9)
- Accumulations of objects according to certain topics or
characteristics that do not necessarily share a common provenance.
- **Concept URI:** http://vocab.terralid.org#c_d0a53b23
- **Other Properties:**

[]{#vocab.terralid.org#c_665746e2}

###  components
- **Child of**:
 [`Konzepte`](#000024)
 [`assemblage`](#vocab.terralid.org#c_0fab0ef9)
- Objects that are intended to be always used or present in connection
with other components, such as a rivet.
- **Concept URI:** http://vocab.terralid.org#c_665746e2
- **Other Properties:**

[]{#vocab.terralid.org#c_5f8d30a4}

###  hoard
- **Child of**:
 [`Konzepte`](#000024)
 [`assemblage`](#vocab.terralid.org#c_0fab0ef9)
- Group of objects that were accumulated with the aim to be buried or
hidden.
- **Concept URI:** http://vocab.terralid.org#c_5f8d30a4
- **Other Properties:**

[]{#vocab.terralid.org#c_5f249aa8}

###  item
- **Child of**:
 [`Konzepte`](#000024)
 [`assemblage`](#vocab.terralid.org#c_0fab0ef9)
- A single object.
- **Concept URI:** http://vocab.terralid.org#c_5f249aa8
- **Other Properties:**

[]{#vocab.terralid.org#c_db7df3e1}

###  set
- **Child of**:
 [`Konzepte`](#000024)
 [`assemblage`](#vocab.terralid.org#c_0fab0ef9)
- Objects purposely combined to be used as group.
- **Concept URI:** http://vocab.terralid.org#c_db7df3e1
- **Other Properties:**

[]{#vocab.terralid.org#c_e6cf20b1}

###  gossan
- **Child of**:
 [`υλικά μορφώματα`](#000019)
 [`assemblage`](#vocab.terralid.org#c_0fab0ef9)
 [`geological material`](#vocab.terralid.org#c_888076c5)
- The outcropping, heavily weathered part of a mineralisation.
- **Concept URI:** http://vocab.terralid.org#c_e6cf20b1
- **Other Properties:**

[]{#vocab.terralid.org#c_b81f7872}

###  alteration product (hydrothermal)
- **Child of**:
 [`materials`](#000003)
 [`assemblage`](#vocab.terralid.org#c_0fab0ef9)
 [`alteration product`](#vocab.terralid.org#c_d5356583)
- A rock whose chemical or mineralogical composition is changed by
hydrothermal solutions.
- **Concept URI:** http://vocab.terralid.org#c_b81f7872
- **Other Properties:**

[]{#vocab.terralid.org#c_f4468f4e}

###  alteration product (supergene)
- **Child of**:
 [`materials`](#000003)
 [`assemblage`](#vocab.terralid.org#c_0fab0ef9)
 [`alteration product`](#vocab.terralid.org#c_d5356583)
- A rock whose chemical or mineralogical composition is changed by
weathering.
- **Concept URI:** http://vocab.terralid.org#c_f4468f4e
- **Other Properties:**

[]{#vocab.terralid.org#c_9c0fc70d}

###  host rock
- **Child of**:
 [`materials`](#000003)
 [`assemblage`](#vocab.terralid.org#c_0fab0ef9)
 [`geological material`](#vocab.terralid.org#c_888076c5)
 [`gangue`](#vocab.terralid.org#c_235c43fb)
- The country rock or rock body in which a mineralisation occurs.
- **Concept URI:** http://vocab.terralid.org#c_9c0fc70d
- **Other Properties:**

[]{#vocab.terralid.org#c_4dffb82b}

###  ore
- **Child of**:
 [`materials`](#000003)
 [`assemblage`](#vocab.terralid.org#c_0fab0ef9)
 [`geological material`](#vocab.terralid.org#c_888076c5)
- The material that is extracted from a mineralisation because of its
economic value.
- **Concept URI:** http://vocab.terralid.org#c_4dffb82b
- **Other Properties:**

[]{#vocab.terralid.org#c_1823779e}

####  primary ore
- **Child of**:
 [`ore`](#vocab.terralid.org#c_4dffb82b)
- The material that is extracted from a mineralisation because of its
economic value that was not altered by hydrothermal processes or
weathering.
- **Concept URI:** http://vocab.terralid.org#c_1823779e
- **Other Properties:**

[]{#vocab.terralid.org#c_adc64d1b}

####  secondary ore
- **Child of**:
 [`ore`](#vocab.terralid.org#c_4dffb82b)
- The material that is extracted from a mineralisation because of its
economic value that was altered by hydrothermal processes or
weathering. The term usually refers to ore that became enriched in the
targeted elements because of alteration processes.
- **Concept URI:** http://vocab.terralid.org#c_adc64d1b
- **Other Properties:**

[]{#vocab.terralid.org#c_27e6e534}

####  alteration
- **Child of**:
 [`ore`](#vocab.terralid.org#c_4dffb82b)
- The part of the ore that is affected by natural post-genetic
processes, often including interaction with a hydrous fluid.
- **Concept URI:** http://vocab.terralid.org#c_27e6e534
- **Other Properties:**

[]{#vocab.terralid.org#c_39a52896}

####  ore mineral
- **Child of**:
 [`ore`](#vocab.terralid.org#c_4dffb82b)
- The economically viable part of the ore.
- **Concept URI:** http://vocab.terralid.org#c_39a52896
- **Other Properties:**

[]{#vocab.terralid.org#c_6e9f3cf6}

#####  major mineral
- **Child of**:
 [`ore mineral`](#vocab.terralid.org#c_39a52896)
- An economically viable mineral dominating the ore's mineralogical
composition.
- **Concept URI:** http://vocab.terralid.org#c_6e9f3cf6
- **Other Properties:**

[]{#vocab.terralid.org#c_42b68c9f}

#####  minor mineral
- **Child of**:
 [`ore mineral`](#vocab.terralid.org#c_39a52896)
- An economically viable mineral constituting a minor part in the
ore's mineralogy.
- **Concept URI:** http://vocab.terralid.org#c_42b68c9f
- **Other Properties:**

[]{#vocab.terralid.org#c_25814b64}

###  wall rock
- **Child of**:
 [`materials`](#000003)
 [`assemblage`](#vocab.terralid.org#c_0fab0ef9)
 [`geological material`](#vocab.terralid.org#c_888076c5)
- The section of the host rock that was affected by epigenetic
processes resulting in the mineralisation.
- **Concept URI:** http://vocab.terralid.org#c_25814b64
- **Other Properties:**

[]{#vocab.terralid.org#c_235c43fb}

###  gangue
- **Child of**:
 [`assemblage`](#vocab.terralid.org#c_0fab0ef9)
- The part of an ore not deemed economically desirable.
- **Concept URI:** http://vocab.terralid.org#c_235c43fb
- **Other Properties:**

[]{#vocab.terralid.org#c_9c0fc70d}

####  host rock
- **Child of**:
 [`materials`](#000003)
 [`assemblage`](#vocab.terralid.org#c_0fab0ef9)
 [`geological material`](#vocab.terralid.org#c_888076c5)
 [`gangue`](#vocab.terralid.org#c_235c43fb)
- The country rock or rock body in which a mineralisation occurs.
- **Concept URI:** http://vocab.terralid.org#c_9c0fc70d
- **Other Properties:**

###  stray find
- **Child of**:
 [`discovery activity`](#vocab.terralid.org#c_e95a9fbf)
- The incidental discovery (and recovery) of materials of interest.
- **Concept URI:** http://vocab.terralid.org#c_84c6c6e0
- **Other Properties:**

[]{#vocab.terralid.org#c_eb09b8cd}

###  borehole survey
- **Child of**:
 [`discovery activity`](#vocab.terralid.org#c_e95a9fbf)
- Drilling into the ground with the aim to collect samples and/or to
describe the stratigraphy and subsurface features of archaeological
and geological features.
- **Concept URI:** http://vocab.terralid.org#c_eb09b8cd
- **Other Properties:**

[]{#vocab.terralid.org#c_410a0d02}

####  core drilling
- **Child of**:
 [`borehole survey`](#vocab.terralid.org#c_eb09b8cd)
- Drilling into the ground with the aim to retrieve the content of the
borehole as a whole. The material will subsequently be used to collect
samples and/or to describe the stratigraphy and subsurface features of
archaeological and geological features.
- **Concept URI:** http://vocab.terralid.org#c_410a0d02
- **Other Properties:**

[]{#vocab.terralid.org#c_19639bab}

####  auger drilling
- **Child of**:
 [`borehole survey`](#vocab.terralid.org#c_eb09b8cd)
- Drilling into the ground with the aim to retrieve the content of the
borehole as loose material to retrieve samples. In some cases, it can
be used to infer stratigraphy and subsurface features of
archaeological and geological features.
- **Concept URI:** http://vocab.terralid.org#c_19639bab
- **Other Properties:**

[]{#vocab.terralid.org#c_3f471934}

###  metal detecting
- **Child of**:
 [`discovery activity`](#vocab.terralid.org#c_e95a9fbf)
- The use of a metal detector to discover artefacts.
- **Concept URI:** http://vocab.terralid.org#c_3f471934
- **Other Properties:**

[]{#vocab.terralid.org#c_cd2a7865}

###  excavation
- **Child of**:
 [`discovery activity`](#vocab.terralid.org#c_e95a9fbf)
- Controlled and documented intrusive fieldwork with the aim to record
an site and to retrieve samples and artefacts from the site.
- **Concept URI:** http://vocab.terralid.org#c_cd2a7865
- **Other Properties:**

[]{#vocab.terralid.org#c_8eff20cd}

####  research excavation
- **Child of**:
 [`excavation`](#vocab.terralid.org#c_cd2a7865)
- Controlled and documented intrusive fieldwork with the aim to answer
a specific research question by recording an site and retrieving
samples and artefacts from the site.
- **Concept URI:** http://vocab.terralid.org#c_8eff20cd
- **Other Properties:**

[]{#vocab.terralid.org#c_f39b79f2}

####  rescue excavation
- **Child of**:
 [`excavation`](#vocab.terralid.org#c_cd2a7865)
- Controlled and documented intrusive fieldwork with the aim to record
an site under threat of destruction and to retrieve samples and
artefacts from the site under strict time constraints.
- **Alternate labels:**
salvage excavation
- **Concept URI:** http://vocab.terralid.org#c_f39b79f2
- **Other Properties:**

[]{#vocab.terralid.org#c_6a6e0a7f}

####  underwater excavation
- **Child of**:
 [`excavation`](#vocab.terralid.org#c_cd2a7865)
- Controlled and documented intrusive fieldwork with the aim to record
an underwater site and to retrieve samples and artefacts from the
site.
- **Concept URI:** http://vocab.terralid.org#c_6a6e0a7f
- **Other Properties:**

[]{#vocab.terralid.org#c_ae4a0583}

###  survey
- **Child of**:
 [`discovery activity`](#vocab.terralid.org#c_e95a9fbf)
- Non-intrusive fieldwork to document the extent and layout of a site
through recording the position and distribution of specimens and
artefacts.
- **Concept URI:** http://vocab.terralid.org#c_ae4a0583
- **Other Properties:**

[]{#vocab.terralid.org#c_2758f3b1}

###  mining
- **Child of**:
 [`discovery activity`](#vocab.terralid.org#c_e95a9fbf)
- The economic extraction of georesources from the earth. This
includes surface and underground operations.
- **Concept URI:** http://vocab.terralid.org#c_2758f3b1
- **Other Properties:**

[]{#vocab.terralid.org#c_77f07bb9}

###  explorative mining activities
- **Child of**:
 [`discovery activity`](#vocab.terralid.org#c_e95a9fbf)
- Mining activities in preparation to establishing a full-scale mining
operation.
- **Concept URI:** http://vocab.terralid.org#c_77f07bb9
- **Other Properties:**

[]{#vocab.terralid.org#c_074a675c}

###  ore prospection
- **Child of**:
 [`discovery activity`](#vocab.terralid.org#c_e95a9fbf)
- The systematic survey on an area with the aim to identify the
presence of potentially economically viable mineralisations.
- **Concept URI:** http://vocab.terralid.org#c_074a675c
- **Other Properties:**

[]{#vocab.terralid.org#c_d0e965f6}

###  illicit digging
- **Child of**:
 [`discovery activity`](#vocab.terralid.org#c_e95a9fbf)
- Uncontrolled and usually undocumented intrusive fieldwork with the
aim to collected artefacts.
- **Alternate labels:**
looting
- **Concept URI:** http://vocab.terralid.org#c_d0e965f6
- **Other Properties:**

[]{#vocab.terralid.org#c_0541320f}

###  undisturbed context
- **Child of**:
 [`context type`](#vocab.terralid.org#c_ca223f0b)
- The context represents a single depositional event with all material
coming from the same source and no interaction happening after its
deposition.
- **Alternate labels:**
primary context
- **Concept URI:** http://vocab.terralid.org#c_0541320f
- **Other Properties:**

[]{#vocab.terralid.org#c_0faa6b29}

###  disturbed context
- **Child of**:
 [`context type`](#vocab.terralid.org#c_ca223f0b)
- The material in the context represents multiple depositional events
or it was interacted with after its deposition.
- **Alternate labels:**
secondary context
- **Concept URI:** http://vocab.terralid.org#c_0faa6b29
- **Other Properties:**

[]{#vocab.terralid.org#c_9408f513}

####  anthropogenically disturbed
- **Child of**:
 [`disturbed context`](#vocab.terralid.org#c_0faa6b29)
- The material in the context represents multiple depositional events
or it was interacted with humans being the responsible agents.
- **Concept URI:** http://vocab.terralid.org#c_9408f513
- **Other Properties:**

[]{#vocab.terralid.org#c_1c4bfb96}

####  biogenically disturbed
- **Child of**:
 [`disturbed context`](#vocab.terralid.org#c_0faa6b29)
- The material in the context represents multiple depositional events
or it was interacted with non-humans being the responsible agents
(e.g. bioturbidation).
- **Concept URI:** http://vocab.terralid.org#c_1c4bfb96
- **Other Properties:**

[]{#vocab.terralid.org#c_a0ae7ce8}

###  mixed context
- **Child of**:
 [`context type`](#vocab.terralid.org#c_ca223f0b)
- The context represents a single depositional event with material
coming from multiple sources and no interaction happening after its
deposition.
- **Concept URI:** http://vocab.terralid.org#c_a0ae7ce8
- **Other Properties:**

[]{#vocab.terralid.org#c_6eeee275}

##  object life cycle stage
- **Child of**:
 [`Konzepte`](#000024)
- The different stages an object undergoes after its collection in the
field.
- **Concept URI:** http://vocab.terralid.org#c_6eeee275
- **Other Properties:**

[]{#vocab.terralid.org#c_20b5f585}

###  storage stage
- **Child of**:
 [`human interactions`](#000011)
 [`object life cycle stage`](#vocab.terralid.org#c_6eeee275)
- The object is stored in a place to keep it safe in the long term.
- **Concept URI:** http://vocab.terralid.org#c_20b5f585
- **Other Properties:**

[]{#vocab.terralid.org#c_084b00a4}

###  restoration stage
- **Child of**:
 [`human interactions`](#000011)
 [`object life cycle stage`](#vocab.terralid.org#c_6eeee275)
- The object is brought back to its original shape as much as
possible, often with addition of missing parts.
- **Concept URI:** http://vocab.terralid.org#c_084b00a4
- **Other Properties:**

[]{#vocab.terralid.org#c_5282dab3}

###  conservation stage
- **Child of**:
 [`human interactions`](#000011)
 [`object life cycle stage`](#vocab.terralid.org#c_6eeee275)
- The object is treated in a conservation laboratory to stop ongoing
deterioation and to prepare it for long-term storage.
- **Concept URI:** http://vocab.terralid.org#c_5282dab3
- **Other Properties:**

[]{#vocab.terralid.org#c_75b32326}

###  finds processing stage
- **Child of**:
 [`human interactions`](#000011)
 [`object life cycle stage`](#vocab.terralid.org#c_6eeee275)
- The object undergoes limited treatment in the field such as washing
or basic convservation measures.
- **Alternate labels:**
in-situ conservation
- **Concept URI:** http://vocab.terralid.org#c_75b32326
- **Other Properties:**

[]{#vocab.terralid.org#c_687c5f00}

###  discovery stage
- **Child of**:
 [`human interactions`](#000011)
 [`object life cycle stage`](#vocab.terralid.org#c_6eeee275)
- The object did not received any treatment (yet) since its discovery.
- **Concept URI:** http://vocab.terralid.org#c_687c5f00
- **Other Properties:**

[]{#vocab.terralid.org#c_33bebd59}

###  laboratory stage
- **Child of**:
 [`environnement bâti`](#000018)
 [`object life cycle stage`](#vocab.terralid.org#c_6eeee275)
- The object is under investigation in a laboratory.
- **Concept URI:** http://vocab.terralid.org#c_33bebd59
- **Other Properties:**

- **Other Properties:**

[]{#vocab.terralid.org#c_d7fb0caf}

- **Child of**:
 [`object material`](#vocab.terralid.org#c_9dd8c641)
 [`anthropogenic material`](#vocab.terralid.org#c_62b205ba)
- The object is (predominantly) made of a nonmetallic malleable

 [`object material`](#vocab.terralid.org#c_9dd8c641)
 [`anthropogenic material`](#vocab.terralid.org#c_62b205ba)

- **Other Properties:**

[]{#vocab.terralid.org#c_068f3adc}

###  metal
- **Child of**:
 [`object material`](#vocab.terralid.org#c_9dd8c641)
 [`anthropogenic material`](#vocab.terralid.org#c_62b205ba)
- The object is (predominantly) made of a metallic material.
- **Concept URI:** http://vocab.terralid.org#c_068f3adc
- **Other Properties:**

[]{#vocab.terralid.org#c_9775890c}

####  coin
- **Child of**:
 [`objetos móveis`](#000017)
 [`metal`](#vocab.terralid.org#c_068f3adc)
- A usually small, flat and round piece of metal primarily used as
medium of exchange or legal tender. It usually follows a standardised
system in weight and sometimes size and is produced in large
quantities.
- **Concept URI:** http://vocab.terralid.org#c_9775890c
- **Other Properties:**

[]{#vocab.terralid.org#c_dbeb733b}

###  ore (TerraLID category)

 [`object material`](#vocab.terralid.org#c_9dd8c641)
- Geological material from which resources are extracted, especially
metals.

####  gossan
- **Child of**:
 [`υλικά μορφώματα`](#000019)
 [`assemblage`](#vocab.terralid.org#c_0fab0ef9)
 [`geological material`](#vocab.terralid.org#c_888076c5)
- The outcropping, heavily weathered part of a mineralisation.
- **Concept URI:** http://vocab.terralid.org#c_e6cf20b1
- **Other Properties:**

[]{#vocab.terralid.org#c_9c0fc70d}

####  host rock
- **Child of**:
 [`materials`](#000003)
 [`assemblage`](#vocab.terralid.org#c_0fab0ef9)
 [`geological material`](#vocab.terralid.org#c_888076c5)
 [`gangue`](#vocab.terralid.org#c_235c43fb)
- The country rock or rock body in which a mineralisation occurs.
- **Concept URI:** http://vocab.terralid.org#c_9c0fc70d
- **Other Properties:**

[]{#vocab.terralid.org#c_4dffb82b}

####  ore
- **Child of**:
 [`materials`](#000003)
 [`assemblage`](#vocab.terralid.org#c_0fab0ef9)
 [`geological material`](#vocab.terralid.org#c_888076c5)
- The material that is extracted from a mineralisation because of its
economic value.
- **Concept URI:** http://vocab.terralid.org#c_4dffb82b
- **Other Properties:**

[]{#vocab.terralid.org#c_1823779e}

#####  primary ore
- **Child of**:
 [`ore`](#vocab.terralid.org#c_4dffb82b)
- The material that is extracted from a mineralisation because of its
economic value that was not altered by hydrothermal processes or
weathering.
- **Concept URI:** http://vocab.terralid.org#c_1823779e
- **Other Properties:**

[]{#vocab.terralid.org#c_adc64d1b}

#####  secondary ore
- **Child of**:
 [`ore`](#vocab.terralid.org#c_4dffb82b)
- The material that is extracted from a mineralisation because of its
economic value that was altered by hydrothermal processes or
weathering. The term usually refers to ore that became enriched in the
targeted elements because of alteration processes.
- **Concept URI:** http://vocab.terralid.org#c_adc64d1b
- **Other Properties:**

[]{#vocab.terralid.org#c_27e6e534}

#####  alteration
- **Child of**:
 [`ore`](#vocab.terralid.org#c_4dffb82b)
- The part of the ore that is affected by natural post-genetic
processes, often including interaction with a hydrous fluid.
- **Concept URI:** http://vocab.terralid.org#c_27e6e534
- **Other Properties:**

[]{#vocab.terralid.org#c_39a52896}

#####  ore mineral
- **Child of**:
 [`ore`](#vocab.terralid.org#c_4dffb82b)
- The economically viable part of the ore.
- **Concept URI:** http://vocab.terralid.org#c_39a52896
- **Other Properties:**

[]{#vocab.terralid.org#c_6e9f3cf6}

######  major mineral
- **Child of**:
 [`ore mineral`](#vocab.terralid.org#c_39a52896)
- An economically viable mineral dominating the ore's mineralogical
composition.
- **Concept URI:** http://vocab.terralid.org#c_6e9f3cf6
- **Other Properties:**

[]{#vocab.terralid.org#c_42b68c9f}

######  minor mineral
- **Child of**:
 [`ore mineral`](#vocab.terralid.org#c_39a52896)
- An economically viable mineral constituting a minor part in the
ore's mineralogy.
- **Concept URI:** http://vocab.terralid.org#c_42b68c9f
- **Other Properties:**

[]{#vocab.terralid.org#c_25814b64}

####  wall rock
- **Child of**:
 [`materials`](#000003)
 [`assemblage`](#vocab.terralid.org#c_0fab0ef9)
 [`geological material`](#vocab.terralid.org#c_888076c5)
- The section of the host rock that was affected by epigenetic
processes resulting in the mineralisation.
- **Concept URI:** http://vocab.terralid.org#c_25814b64
- **Other Properties:**

[]{#vocab.terralid.org#c_fc64d08c}

####  sediment
- **Child of**:
 [`object material`](#vocab.terralid.org#c_9dd8c641)
 [`geological material`](#vocab.terralid.org#c_888076c5)
- The object is unconsolidated geogenic material that was deposited by
water, wind, or ice.
- **Concept URI:** http://vocab.terralid.org#c_fc64d08c
- **Other Properties:**

[]{#vocab.terralid.org#c_b5560869}

####  mineral
- **Child of**:
 [`geological material`](#vocab.terralid.org#c_888076c5)
- A crystalline component of the material with a fairly well-defined
crystal structure and chemical composition.
- **Concept URI:** http://vocab.terralid.org#c_b5560869
- **Other Properties:**

[]{#vocab.terralid.org#c_3a384c38}

####  unaltered material
- **Child of**:
 [`geological material`](#vocab.terralid.org#c_888076c5)
 [`anthropogenic material`](#vocab.terralid.org#c_62b205ba)
- Anthropogenic material without any perceivable impact from
interaction prosesses with the (depositional) environment.
- **Concept URI:** http://vocab.terralid.org#c_3a384c38
- **Other Properties:**

[]{#vocab.terralid.org#c_62b205ba}

###  anthropogenic material
- **Child of**:
 [`sample material`](#vocab.terralid.org#c_dd76eaab)
- Material that was created by humans.
- **Concept URI:** http://vocab.terralid.org#c_62b205ba
- **Other Properties:**

[]{#vocab.terralid.org#c_d7fb0caf}

####  ceramic
- **Child of**:
 [`object material`](#vocab.terralid.org#c_9dd8c641)
 [`anthropogenic material`](#vocab.terralid.org#c_62b205ba)
- The object is (predominantly) made of a nonmetallic malleable
material that was intentionally transformed at high temperatures into
a non-malleable state.
- **Concept URI:** http://vocab.terralid.org#c_d7fb0caf
- **Other Properties:**

[]{#vocab.terralid.org#c_461a3e6b}

####  pigment
- **Child of**:
 [`object material`](#vocab.terralid.org#c_9dd8c641)
 [`anthropogenic material`](#vocab.terralid.org#c_62b205ba)
- The term is used rather loosely here to describe any material that
is used for colouring another material.
- **Alternate labels:**
dye, 
paint, 
- **Concept URI:** http://vocab.terralid.org#c_461a3e6b
- **Other Properties:**

[]{#vocab.terralid.org#c_ee038e9e}

####  glass
- **Child of**:
 [`object material`](#vocab.terralid.org#c_9dd8c641)
 [`anthropogenic material`](#vocab.terralid.org#c_62b205ba)
- The object is (predominantly) made of an inorganic vitrous material
based on fused silica.
- **Concept URI:** http://vocab.terralid.org#c_ee038e9e
- **Other Properties:**

[]{#vocab.terralid.org#c_068f3adc}

####  metal
- **Child of**:
 [`object material`](#vocab.terralid.org#c_9dd8c641)
 [`anthropogenic material`](#vocab.terralid.org#c_62b205ba)
- The object is (predominantly) made of a metallic material.
- **Concept URI:** http://vocab.terralid.org#c_068f3adc
- **Other Properties:**

[]{#vocab.terralid.org#c_9775890c}

#####  coin
- **Child of**:
 [`objetos móveis`](#000017)
 [`metal`](#vocab.terralid.org#c_068f3adc)
- A usually small, flat and round piece of metal primarily used as
medium of exchange or legal tender. It usually follows a standardised
system in weight and sometimes size and is produced in large
quantities.
- **Concept URI:** http://vocab.terralid.org#c_9775890c
- **Other Properties:**

[]{#vocab.terralid.org#c_1e35bdea}

####  alteration product (anthropogenic)
- **Child of**:
 [`anthropogenic material`](#vocab.terralid.org#c_62b205ba)
 [`alteration product`](#vocab.terralid.org#c_d5356583)
- Anthropogenic material that is impacted by interaction processes
with the (depositional) environment.
- **Concept URI:** http://vocab.terralid.org#c_1e35bdea
- **Other Properties:**

[]{#vocab.terralid.org#c_3a384c38}

####  unaltered material
- **Child of**:
 [`geological material`](#vocab.terralid.org#c_888076c5)
 [`anthropogenic material`](#vocab.terralid.org#c_62b205ba)
- Anthropogenic material without any perceivable impact from
interaction prosesses with the (depositional) environment.
- **Concept URI:** http://vocab.terralid.org#c_3a384c38
- **Other Properties:**

[]{#vocab.terralid.org#c_d5356583}

###  alteration product
- **Child of**:
 [`sample material`](#vocab.terralid.org#c_dd76eaab)
- Material that was transformed by the interaction of its pristine
state with the (depositional) environment.
- **Concept URI:** http://vocab.terralid.org#c_d5356583
- **Other Properties:**

[]{#vocab.terralid.org#c_b81f7872}

####  alteration product (hydrothermal)
- **Child of**:
 [`materials`](#000003)
 [`assemblage`](#vocab.terralid.org#c_0fab0ef9)
 [`alteration product`](#vocab.terralid.org#c_d5356583)
- A rock whose chemical or mineralogical composition is changed by
hydrothermal solutions.
- **Concept URI:** http://vocab.terralid.org#c_b81f7872
- **Other Properties:**

[]{#vocab.terralid.org#c_f4468f4e}

####  alteration product (supergene)
- **Child of**:
 [`materials`](#000003)
 [`assemblage`](#vocab.terralid.org#c_0fab0ef9)
 [`alteration product`](#vocab.terralid.org#c_d5356583)
- A rock whose chemical or mineralogical composition is changed by
weathering.
- **Concept URI:** http://vocab.terralid.org#c_f4468f4e
- **Other Properties:**

[]{#vocab.terralid.org#c_1e35bdea}

####  alteration product (anthropogenic)
- **Child of**:
 [`anthropogenic material`](#vocab.terralid.org#c_62b205ba)
 [`alteration product`](#vocab.terralid.org#c_d5356583)
- Anthropogenic material that is impacted by interaction processes
with the (depositional) environment.
- **Concept URI:** http://vocab.terralid.org#c_1e35bdea
- **Other Properties:**

[]{#vocab.terralid.org#c_d339fac4}

###  bulk
- **Child of**:
 [`sample material`](#vocab.terralid.org#c_dd76eaab)
- A fraction of the material that represents the average composition
of the material.
- **Concept URI:** http://vocab.terralid.org#c_d339fac4
- **Other Properties:**

###  drill shavings
- **Child of**:
 [`sample type`](#vocab.terralid.org#c_f8762e3d)
- The sample was obtained with a rotary tool and is present in larger
pieces.
- **Concept URI:** http://vocab.terralid.org#c_bfd0ca9e
- **Other Properties:**

[]{#vocab.terralid.org#c_1a77d68d}

###  in-situ
- **Child of**:
 [`sample type`](#vocab.terralid.org#c_f8762e3d)
- The analysis was carried out directly on the object without
collecting a separate sample.
- **Concept URI:** http://vocab.terralid.org#c_1a77d68d
- **Other Properties:**

[]{#vocab.terralid.org#c_153bc01e}

###  powder
- **Child of**:
 [`sample type`](#vocab.terralid.org#c_f8762e3d)
- A powder was obtained during sampling or sampled material was
subsequently ground to powder.
- **Concept URI:** http://vocab.terralid.org#c_153bc01e
- **Other Properties:**

[]{#vocab.terralid.org#c_26707627}

###  hand-picked
- **Child of**:
 [`sample type`](#vocab.terralid.org#c_f8762e3d)
- A part of the object was crushed and the sample manually separated
after visual inspection of each fragment.
- **Concept URI:** http://vocab.terralid.org#c_26707627
- **Other Properties:**

[]{#vocab.terralid.org#c_0118bbdc}

###  mineral separate
- **Child of**:
 [`sample type`](#vocab.terralid.org#c_f8762e3d)
- A part of the object was crushed and the sample extracted based on
its physical or chemical properties (e.g., density, magnetic
properties).
- **Concept URI:** http://vocab.terralid.org#c_0118bbdc
- **Other Properties:**

[]{#vocab.terralid.org#c_fb28d155}

###  leachate
- **Child of**:
 [`sample type`](#vocab.terralid.org#c_f8762e3d)
- Acids or other solvents were applied to a usually crushed part of
the object to extract the sample as solution. Usually only done with
geological material.
- **Concept URI:** http://vocab.terralid.org#c_fb28d155
- **Other Properties:**

[]{#vocab.terralid.org#c_07f899ac}

###  swabbed material
- **Child of**:
 [`sample type`](#vocab.terralid.org#c_f8762e3d)
- Acids or other solvents are applied with a soft and absorbent tool
(e.g., Q-tip, sponge) on the object to extract the sample as solution
absorbed to the same tool and/or particles adhering to it. Usually
only carried out on archaeological materials.
- **Concept URI:** http://vocab.terralid.org#c_07f899ac
- **Other Properties:**

[]{#vocab.terralid.org#c_d8e9f67e}

###  hand specimen fragments
- **Child of**:
 [`sample type`](#vocab.terralid.org#c_f8762e3d)
- The object was crushed in to pieces, which were then taken as
sample. This is usually only done with larger items of geological
origin such as rocks.
- **Concept URI:** http://vocab.terralid.org#c_d8e9f67e
- **Other Properties:**

[]{#vocab.terralid.org#c_d57dc599}

###  loose material
- **Child of**:
 [`sample type`](#vocab.terralid.org#c_f8762e3d)
- The object already had detached pieces, which were collected as
sample.
- **Concept URI:** http://vocab.terralid.org#c_d57dc599
- **Other Properties:**

[]{#vocab.terralid.org#c_a7cdeda3}

###  cut
- **Child of**:
 [`sample type`](#vocab.terralid.org#c_f8762e3d)
- The sample is cut from the object with e.g. a blade or saw.
- **Concept URI:** http://vocab.terralid.org#c_a7cdeda3
- **Other Properties:**

[]{#vocab.terralid.org#c_fb91df68}

###  section
- **Child of**:
 [`sample type`](#vocab.terralid.org#c_f8762e3d)
- A larger piece of the object is cut with the aim to create a section
of it, usually for inspection with a microscope and/or if the object
is too large to fit into the analytical instrument's sample chamber.
- **Concept URI:** http://vocab.terralid.org#c_fb91df68
- **Other Properties:**

[]{#vocab.terralid.org#c_d33a496b}

####  polished section
- **Child of**:
 [`section`](#vocab.terralid.org#c_fb91df68)
- The section is polished to an even surface.
- **Concept URI:** http://vocab.terralid.org#c_d33a496b
- **Other Properties:**

[]{#vocab.terralid.org#c_4365b275}

####  thin section
- **Child of**:
 [`section`](#vocab.terralid.org#c_fb91df68)
- The section is cut and polished to a thickness that it becomes
translucent.
- **Concept URI:** http://vocab.terralid.org#c_4365b275
- **Other Properties:**

###  drilling
- **Child of**:
 [`sampling method`](#vocab.terralid.org#c_2e6c0e69)
- The use of a rotating tool to remove material.
- **Concept URI:** http://vocab.terralid.org#c_961ffe7e
- **Other Properties:**

[]{#vocab.terralid.org#c_e06764b7}

####  micromill
- **Child of**:
 [`drilling`](#vocab.terralid.org#c_961ffe7e)
- A rotary tool equipped with a camera that allows drilling on the
micrometre scale in high spatial resolution.
- **Concept URI:** http://vocab.terralid.org#c_e06764b7
- **Other Properties:**

[]{#vocab.terralid.org#c_a5e29dda}

###  cutting
- **Child of**:
 [`sampling method`](#vocab.terralid.org#c_2e6c0e69)
- The use of a blade or saw to remove a chunk from the sample.
- **Concept URI:** http://vocab.terralid.org#c_a5e29dda
- **Other Properties:**

[]{#vocab.terralid.org#c_3cc85343}

###  abrading
- **Child of**:
 [`sampling method`](#vocab.terralid.org#c_2e6c0e69)
- The mechanical removal of material by applying material with a rough
surface and higher hardness than the object.
- **Concept URI:** http://vocab.terralid.org#c_3cc85343
- **Other Properties:**

[]{#vocab.terralid.org#c_89610abe}

###  ablating
- **Child of**:
 [`sampling method`](#vocab.terralid.org#c_2e6c0e69)
- The removal of material with a high energy beam such as a laser.
- **Alternate labels:**
laser ablation
- **Concept URI:** http://vocab.terralid.org#c_89610abe
- **Other Properties:**

[]{#vocab.terralid.org#c_959163ef}

###  dissolution
- **Child of**:
 [`sampling method`](#vocab.terralid.org#c_2e6c0e69)
- The removal of material with an acid or solvent.
- **Concept URI:** http://vocab.terralid.org#c_959163ef
- **Other Properties:**

[]{#vocab.terralid.org#c_15912994}

####  swabbing
- **Child of**:
 [`dissolution`](#vocab.terralid.org#c_959163ef)
- The dissolution of material with the solvent or acid being applied
with a spongy and/or absorbent tissue, usually on a small short stick.
- **Alternate labels:**
Q-tip, 
sponge, 
- **Concept URI:** http://vocab.terralid.org#c_15912994
- **Other Properties:**

[]{#vocab.terralid.org#c_02b29aae}

####  etching/leaching
- **Child of**:
 [`dissolution`](#vocab.terralid.org#c_959163ef)
- The dissolution of specific parts of a material by covering or
submerging it in an acid or solvent. While etching only affects the
surface, leaching affects the entire sample.
- **Concept URI:** http://vocab.terralid.org#c_02b29aae
- **Other Properties:**

[]{#vocab.terralid.org#c_64e8e4be}

###  fragment collection
- **Child of**:
 [`sampling method`](#vocab.terralid.org#c_2e6c0e69)
- The collection of material that was already detached from the
object.
- **Concept URI:** http://vocab.terralid.org#c_64e8e4be
- **Other Properties:**

[]{#vocab.terralid.org#c_9b8c9c7e}

###  crushing
- **Child of**:
 [`sampling method`](#vocab.terralid.org#c_2e6c0e69)
- The fragmentation of a material by applying mechanical force to it
until it breals.
- **Concept URI:** http://vocab.terralid.org#c_9b8c9c7e
- **Other Properties:**

[]{#vocab.terralid.org#c_3eae3e76}

###  separation
- **Child of**:
 [`sampling method`](#vocab.terralid.org#c_2e6c0e69)
- The division of material into its components according to the
components' properties.
- **Concept URI:** http://vocab.terralid.org#c_3eae3e76
- **Other Properties:**

[]{#vocab.terralid.org#c_852bd94d}

####  magnet separation
- **Child of**:
 [`separation`](#vocab.terralid.org#c_3eae3e76)
- The separation process is based on the material components' magnetic
properties.
- **Concept URI:** http://vocab.terralid.org#c_852bd94d
- **Other Properties:**

[]{#vocab.terralid.org#c_5eab7b34}

[]{#vocab.terralid.org#c_c1c261a2}

##  analytical method
- **Child of**:
 [`méthodes`](#000023)
- Procedures that operate on material samples to produce observation
results with information about the physical properties, chemical or
isotopic composition, crystallography, or molecular structure of the
sample.
- **Concept URI:** http://vocab.terralid.org#c_c1c261a2
- **Other Properties:**

[]{#vocab.terralid.org#c_c0d215f3}

###  mass spectrometer
- **Child of**:
 [`analytical method`](#vocab.terralid.org#c_c1c261a2)
- An analytical instrument in which the sample is ionised, the ions
separated and detected according to their mass-to-charge ratio.
- **Concept URI:** http://vocab.terralid.org#c_c0d215f3
- **Other Properties:**

[]{#vocab.terralid.org#c_e7d3223e}

####  TIMS
- **Child of**:
 [`mass spectrometer`](#vocab.terralid.org#c_c0d215f3)
- Mass spectrometer in which the sample is placed on a filament and
ionised by heating the filament. With respect to lead isotope
analyses, mass detection by a detector array in implied.
- **Concept URI:** http://vocab.terralid.org#c_e7d3223e
- **Other Properties:**

[]{#vocab.terralid.org#c_32908a70}

####  ICP-MS
- **Child of**:
 [`mass spectrometer`](#vocab.terralid.org#c_c0d215f3)
- Mass spectrometry technique in which the sample is introduced into
an inductively coupled plasma to atomize and ionize the sample for
inlet to mass analyzer.
- **Concept URI:** http://vocab.terralid.org#c_32908a70
- **Other Properties:**

[]{#vocab.terralid.org#c_d0d46a2c}

#####  MC-ICP-MS
- **Child of**:
 [`ICP-MS`](#vocab.terralid.org#c_32908a70)
- Mass spectrometer in which the sample is ionised by an inductively
coupled plasma and ions detected by an array of several detectors.
- **Concept URI:** http://vocab.terralid.org#c_d0d46a2c
- **Other Properties:**

[]{#vocab.terralid.org#c_7b1b4216}

######  LA-MC-ICP-MS
- **Child of**:
 [`MC-ICP-MS`](#vocab.terralid.org#c_d0d46a2c)
 [`LA-ICP-MS`](#vocab.terralid.org#c_6b7986e4)
- Mass spectrometer in which the sample is ablated with a laser beam,
ionised by an inductively coupled plasma and ions detected by an array
of several detectors.
- **Concept URI:** http://vocab.terralid.org#c_7b1b4216
- **Other Properties:**

[]{#vocab.terralid.org#c_e56f0531}

#######  fs-LA-MC-ICP-MS

 [`LA-MC-ICP-MS`](#vocab.terralid.org#c_7b1b4216)
- Mass spectrometer in which the sample is ablated with a laser beam
with laser pulses in the femtosecond range, ionised by an inductively
coupled plasma and ions detected by an array of several detectors.
- **Concept URI:** http://vocab.terralid.org#c_e56f0531
- **Other Properties:**

[]{#vocab.terralid.org#c_e1700c12}

 [`LA-MC-ICP-MS`](#vocab.terralid.org#c_7b1b4216)

with laser pulses in the nanosecond range, ionised by an inductively
coupled plasma and ions detected by an array of several detectors.
- **Concept URI:** http://vocab.terralid.org#c_e1700c12
- **Other Properties:**

[]{#vocab.terralid.org#c_8e269b3e}

- **Child of**:

###  atomic absorption spectroscopy
- **Child of**:
 [`analytical method`](#vocab.terralid.org#c_c1c261a2)
- Analytical methods using the characteristic optical absorption of
chemical elements for identification and quantification.
- **Concept URI:** http://vocab.terralid.org#c_d1ac5ad6
- **Other Properties:**

[]{#vocab.terralid.org#c_ea3b1b8c}

####  AAS (glow-discharge)
- **Child of**:
 [`atomic absorption spectroscopy`](#vocab.terralid.org#c_d1ac5ad6)
- Determination of the chemical composition by the optical absorption
of a heated samples. Heat is induced by glow-discharge.
- **Concept URI:** http://vocab.terralid.org#c_ea3b1b8c
- **Other Properties:**

[]{#vocab.terralid.org#c_d7db2dd2}

####  AAS (flame)
- **Child of**:
 [`atomic absorption spectroscopy`](#vocab.terralid.org#c_d1ac5ad6)
- Determination of the chemical composition by the optical absorption
of a heated samples. Heat is induced by a flame.
- **Concept URI:** http://vocab.terralid.org#c_d7db2dd2
- **Other Properties:**

[]{#vocab.terralid.org#c_8d344a67}

####  AAS (graphite furnace)
- **Child of**:
 [`atomic absorption spectroscopy`](#vocab.terralid.org#c_d1ac5ad6)
- Determination of the chemical composition by the optical absorption
of a heated samples. Heat is induced by a graphite furnace.
- **Alternate labels:**
ET-AAS
- **Concept URI:** http://vocab.terralid.org#c_8d344a67
- **Other Properties:**

[]{#vocab.terralid.org#c_3d79fcf3}

- **Child of**:
 [`analytical method`](#vocab.terralid.org#c_c1c261a2)
- Analytical methods using the characteristic optical emission of

- **Other Properties:**

[]{#vocab.terralid.org#c_3687738e}

####  OES (spark)
- **Child of**:
 [`emission spectrometry`](#vocab.terralid.org#c_3d79fcf3)
- Determination of the chemical composition by optimal emission of a
heated sample. Heat is induced through an electric spark.
- **Concept URI:** http://vocab.terralid.org#c_3687738e
- **Other Properties:**

[]{#vocab.terralid.org#c_67246a66}

####  OES (ICP)
- **Child of**:
 [`emission spectrometry`](#vocab.terralid.org#c_3d79fcf3)
- Determination of the chemical composition by optimal emission of a
heated sample. Sample is heated in a inductively coupled plasma.
- **Alternate labels:**
AES (ICP)
- **Concept URI:** http://vocab.terralid.org#c_67246a66
- **Other Properties:**

[]{#vocab.terralid.org#c_0a6e5db7}

####  AES (graphite furnace)
- **Child of**:
 [`emission spectrometry`](#vocab.terralid.org#c_3d79fcf3)
- Determination of the chemical composition by optimal emission of a
heated sample. Sample is heated in a graphite furnace.
- **Concept URI:** http://vocab.terralid.org#c_0a6e5db7
- **Other Properties:**

[]{#vocab.terralid.org#c_6c7c72d4}

- **Child of**:
 [`emission spectrometry`](#vocab.terralid.org#c_3d79fcf3)
- Determination of the chemical composition by optimal emission of a
heated sample. Heat is induced by a flame.
- **Concept URI:** http://vocab.terralid.org#c_6c7c72d4

hhXRF

SEM-EDX

###  wet chemistry
- **Child of**:
 [`analytical method`](#vocab.terralid.org#c_c1c261a2)
- Determination of the chemical composition by traditional chemical
methods.
- **Concept URI:** http://vocab.terralid.org#c_4bf47ef2
- **Other Properties:**

[]{#vocab.terralid.org#c_4a54f6dc}

###  NAA
- **Child of**:
 [`analytical method`](#vocab.terralid.org#c_c1c261a2)
- The determination of chemical concentrations from the radioactivity
produced by the sample material after its irradiation with neutrons
through comparison with the radioactivity measured in standard
materials of comparable sample matrix.
- **Alternate labels:**
neutron activation analysis
- **Concept URI:** http://vocab.terralid.org#c_4a54f6dc
- **Other Properties:**


[]{#vocab.terralid.org#c_5f1716a4}

##  mass bias correction
- **Child of**:
 [`méthodes`](#000023)
- The mathematical model or procedure used to correct for the
fractionation of an isotope ratio during measurement.
- **Concept URI:** http://vocab.terralid.org#c_5f1716a4
- **Other Properties:**

[]{#vocab.terralid.org#c_39d3b302}

###  linear model
- **Child of**:
 [`mass bias correction`](#vocab.terralid.org#c_5f1716a4)
- A mathematical model assuming a linear relationship between the
isotope ratio of the sample and a different isotope ratio of an
internal standard added to the sample.
- **Concept URI:** http://vocab.terralid.org#c_39d3b302
- **Other Properties:**

[]{#vocab.terralid.org#c_7b213d83}

###  double-spike
- **Child of**:
 [`mass bias correction`](#vocab.terralid.org#c_5f1716a4)
- The correction of an isotope ratio's mass bias by altering the
abundance of two other isotopes of the same element through the
addition of an enriched isotopic spike
- **Concept URI:** http://vocab.terralid.org#c_7b213d83
- **Other Properties:**

[]{#vocab.terralid.org#c_725495a8}

###  standard-sample bracketing
- **Child of**:
 [`mass bias correction`](#vocab.terralid.org#c_5f1716a4)
- The correction of an isotope ratio's mass bias in a sample by
extrapolation from measurements of the same isotope ratio in an
external standard measured before and after the sample.
- **Concept URI:** http://vocab.terralid.org#c_725495a8
- **Other Properties:**

[]{#vocab.terralid.org#c_17be86eb}

###  power law
- **Child of**:
 [`mass bias correction`](#vocab.terralid.org#c_5f1716a4)
- A mathematical model assuming a power-relationship between the
isotope ratio of the sample and a different isotope ratio of an
internal standard added to the sample.
- **Concept URI:** http://vocab.terralid.org#c_17be86eb
- **Other Properties:**

[]{#vocab.terralid.org#c_e9b3f7f1}

###  exponential law
- **Child of**:
 [`mass bias correction`](#vocab.terralid.org#c_5f1716a4)
- A mathematical model assuming an exponential relationship between
the isotope ratio of the sample and a different isotope ratio of an
internal standard added to the sample.
- **Alternate labels:**
Russell model
- **Concept URI:** http://vocab.terralid.org#c_e9b3f7f1
- **Other Properties:**


[]{#vocab.terralid.org#c_c7d45a2d}

##  reference material
- **Child of**:
 [`materials`](#000003)
- A reference material (RM) is a material that is sufficiently stable
and homogeneous in one or more of its properties and whose use for the
calibration of measurements has been established. A certified
reference material (CRM) is characterised by metrologically valid
procedures for one or more specified properties and is accompanied by
a certificate for this characterisation. Standard reference material"
(SRM) is a trademarked term for CRMs satisfying additional criteria
specified by the United States National Institute of Standards and
Technology (NIST).
- **Concept URI:** http://vocab.terralid.org#c_c7d45a2d
- **Other Properties:**

##  lead isotope age model
- **Child of**:
 [`Konzepte`](#000024)
- **Concept URI:** http://vocab.terralid.org#c_435c9bf2
- **Other Properties:**

[]{#vocab.terralid.org#c_10c030b1}

###  SK75
- **Child of**:
 [`lead isotope age model`](#vocab.terralid.org#c_435c9bf2)
- The two-stage lead isotope age model according to Stacey, J.S. and
Kramers, J.D. (1975) Approximation of terrestrial lead isotope
evolution by a two-stage model. Earth and Planetary Science Letters
26(2), pp. 207–221. https://dx.doi.org/10.1016/0012-821X(75)90088-6
- **Concept URI:** http://vocab.terralid.org#c_10c030b1
- **Other Properties:**

[]{#vocab.terralid.org#c_b80ae0f4}

###  CR75
- **Child of**:
 [`lead isotope age model`](#vocab.terralid.org#c_435c9bf2)
- The single-stage lead isotope age model according to Cumming, G.L.
and Richards, J.R. (1975) Ore lead isotope ratios in a continuously
changing earth. Earth and Planetary Science Letters 28(2), pp.
155–171. https://dx.doi.org/10.1016/0012-821X(75)90223-X
- **Concept URI:** http://vocab.terralid.org#c_b80ae0f4
- **Other Properties:**

[]{#vocab.terralid.org#c_a299bc22}

###  AJ84
- **Child of**:
 [`lead isotope age model`](#vocab.terralid.org#c_435c9bf2)
- The two-stage lead isotope age model according to Albarède, F. and
Juteau, M. (1984) Unscrambling the lead model ages. Geochimica et
Cosmochimica Acta 48(1), pp. 207–212.
https://dx.doi.org/10.1016/0016-7037(84)90364-8
- **Concept URI:** http://vocab.terralid.org#c_a299bc22
- **Other Properties:**

[]{#vocab.terralid.org#c_60b698fb}

##  abundance (concentration)
- **Child of**:
 [`υλικά μορφώματα`](#000019)
- A qualitative classification for the quantity of a compund based on
its concentration.
- **Concept URI:** http://vocab.terralid.org#c_60b698fb
- **Other Properties:**

[]{#vocab.terralid.org#c_0e342dcb}

###  major
- **Child of**:
 [`abundance (concentration)`](#vocab.terralid.org#c_60b698fb)
- Compound with a concentration of more than 0.4 wt%.
- **Concept URI:** http://vocab.terralid.org#c_0e342dcb
- **Other Properties:**

[]{#vocab.terralid.org#c_e954a190}

###  minor
- **Child of**:
 [`abundance (concentration)`](#vocab.terralid.org#c_60b698fb)
- Compound with a concentration between 0.4 and 0.1 wt%.
- **Concept URI:** http://vocab.terralid.org#c_e954a190
- **Other Properties:**

[]{#vocab.terralid.org#c_29d78b2d}

###  trace
- **Child of**:
 [`abundance (concentration)`](#vocab.terralid.org#c_60b698fb)
- Compound with a concentration of less than 0.1 wt%.
- **Concept URI:** http://vocab.terralid.org#c_29d78b2d
- **Other Properties:**

###  severe alteration

 [`alteration extent`](#vocab.terralid.org#c_4e14cebd)

[]{#vocab.terralid.org#c_e935826d}

###  slight alteration

 [`alteration extent`](#vocab.terralid.org#c_4e14cebd)
- The material is altered but includes enough pristine material for
analyses.
- **Concept URI:** http://vocab.terralid.org#c_e935826d
- **Other Properties:**

[]{#vocab.terralid.org#c_eb1223d0}

###  no alteration

 [`alteration extent`](#vocab.terralid.org#c_4e14cebd)

[]{#vocab.terralid.org#c_fdeb0d6c}

###  flaking
- **Child of**:
 [`alteration process`](#vocab.terralid.org#c_8469dc11)
- The loss of adhesion of paint from its substrate often but not
necessarily in response to changes in the environment like humidity or
temperature.
- **Concept URI:** http://vocab.terralid.org#c_fdeb0d6c
- **Other Properties:**


[]{#vocab.terralid.org#c_49d4f3c8}

##  colour system
- Definition of a geometrical space or nomenclature that represents
colours.
- **Concept URI:** http://vocab.terralid.org#c_49d4f3c8
- **Other Properties:**

[]{#vocab.terralid.org#c_63dd4d16}

###  eye
- **Child of**:
 [`colour system`](#vocab.terralid.org#c_49d4f3c8)
- The colour as perceived by a person.
- **Concept URI:** http://vocab.terralid.org#c_63dd4d16
- **Other Properties:**

[]{#vocab.terralid.org#c_ffd62f45}

###  Munsell
- **Child of**:
 [`colour system`](#vocab.terralid.org#c_49d4f3c8)
- A colour system developed by A. H. Munsell in the early 20th
century.
- **Concept URI:** http://vocab.terralid.org#c_ffd62f45
- **Other Properties:**

[]{#vocab.terralid.org#c_4f63611c}

###  CIELAB
- **Child of**:
 [`colour system`](#vocab.terralid.org#c_49d4f3c8)
- A colour system defined by the International Commission on
Illumination, expressing colour in a three-dimensional space with
lightness (L*), red-green (A*), and blue-yellow (b*) as axes.

L*a*b*
- **Concept URI:** http://vocab.terralid.org#c_4f63611c

[]{#vocab.terralid.org#c_e4ed600b}

##  contributorType
- **Child of**:
 [`roles of individuals`](#0000074)
- The type of contributor of the resource.

- **Other Properties:**

[]{#vocab.terralid.org#c_3a208ae6}

###  Other (contributorType)
- **Child of**:
 [`contributorType`](#vocab.terralid.org#c_e4ed600b)
- Any person or institution making a significant contribution to the
development and/or maintenance of the resource, but whose contribution
is not adequately described by any of the other values for
contributorType.
- **Concept URI:** http://vocab.terralid.org#c_3a208ae6
- **Other Properties:**

[]{#vocab.terralid.org#c_50da8fc6}

###  WorkPackageLeader
- **Child of**:
 [`contributorType`](#vocab.terralid.org#c_e4ed600b)
- A Work Package is a recognized data product, not all of which is
included in publication. The package, instead, may include notes,
discarded documents, etc. The Work Package Leader is responsible for
ensuring the comprehensive contents, versioning, and availability of
the Work Package during the development of the resource.
- **Concept URI:** http://vocab.terralid.org#c_50da8fc6
- **Other Properties:**

[]{#vocab.terralid.org#c_79574dab}

###  Translator
- **Child of**:
 [`contributorType`](#vocab.terralid.org#c_e4ed600b)
- A person, organization, or automated system responsible for
converting the content of a resource from one language into another,
preserving its meaning and intended message.
- **Concept URI:** http://vocab.terralid.org#c_79574dab
- **Other Properties:**

[]{#vocab.terralid.org#c_2f9a64df}

###  Supervisor
- **Child of**:
 [`contributorType`](#vocab.terralid.org#c_e4ed600b)
- Designated administrator over one or more groups/teams working to
produce a resource, or over one or more steps of a development
process.
- **Concept URI:** http://vocab.terralid.org#c_2f9a64df
- **Other Properties:**

[]{#vocab.terralid.org#c_8ff2aeb0}

###  Sponsor
- **Child of**:
 [`contributorType`](#vocab.terralid.org#c_e4ed600b)
- Person or organisation that issued a contract or under the auspices
of which a work has been written, printed, published, developed, etc.
- **Concept URI:** http://vocab.terralid.org#c_8ff2aeb0
- **Other Properties:**

[]{#vocab.terralid.org#c_f18d65a2}

###  RightsHolder
- **Child of**:
 [`contributorType`](#vocab.terralid.org#c_e4ed600b)
- Person or institution owning or managing property rights, including
intellectual property rights over the resource.
- **Concept URI:** http://vocab.terralid.org#c_f18d65a2
- **Other Properties:**

[]{#vocab.terralid.org#c_57b3dd9a}

###  ResearchGroup
- **Child of**:
 [`contributorType`](#vocab.terralid.org#c_e4ed600b)
- Typically refers to a group of individuals with a lab, department,
or division that has a specifically defined focus of activity.
- **Concept URI:** http://vocab.terralid.org#c_57b3dd9a
- **Other Properties:**

[]{#vocab.terralid.org#c_e1f2cf05}

###  Researcher
- **Child of**:
 [`contributorType`](#vocab.terralid.org#c_e4ed600b)
- A person involved in analysing data or the results of an experiment
or formal study. May indicate an intern or assistant to one of the
authors who helped with research but who was not so “key” as to be
listed as an author.
- **Concept URI:** http://vocab.terralid.org#c_e1f2cf05
- **Other Properties:**

[]{#vocab.terralid.org#c_ad00bdad}

###  RelatedPerson
- **Child of**:
 [`contributorType`](#vocab.terralid.org#c_e4ed600b)
- A person without a specifically defined role in the development of
the resource, but who is someone the author wishes to recognize.
- **Concept URI:** http://vocab.terralid.org#c_ad00bdad
- **Other Properties:**

[]{#vocab.terralid.org#c_ebc3e072}

###  RegistrationAuthority
- **Child of**:
 [`contributorType`](#vocab.terralid.org#c_e4ed600b)
- A standards-setting body from which Registration Agencies obtain
official recognition and guidance.
- **Concept URI:** http://vocab.terralid.org#c_ebc3e072
- **Other Properties:**

[]{#vocab.terralid.org#c_c8f80f6c}

###  RegistrationAgency
- **Child of**:
 [`contributorType`](#vocab.terralid.org#c_e4ed600b)
- Institution/organisation officially appointed by a Registration
Authority to handle specific tasks within a defined area of
responsibility.
- **Concept URI:** http://vocab.terralid.org#c_c8f80f6c
- **Other Properties:**

[]{#vocab.terralid.org#c_c6bb8064}

###  ProjectMember
- **Child of**:
 [`contributorType`](#vocab.terralid.org#c_e4ed600b)
- Person on the membership list of a designated project/project team.
- **Concept URI:** http://vocab.terralid.org#c_c6bb8064
- **Other Properties:**

[]{#vocab.terralid.org#c_384d11d1}

###  ProjectManager
- **Child of**:
 [`contributorType`](#vocab.terralid.org#c_e4ed600b)
- Person officially designated as manager of a project. Project may
consist of one or many project teams and sub-teams.
- **Concept URI:** http://vocab.terralid.org#c_384d11d1
- **Other Properties:**

[]{#vocab.terralid.org#c_dd32fd29}

###  ProjectLeader
- **Child of**:
 [`contributorType`](#vocab.terralid.org#c_e4ed600b)
- Person officially designated as head of project team or sub- project
team instrumental in the work necessary to development of the
resource.
- **Concept URI:** http://vocab.terralid.org#c_dd32fd29
- **Other Properties:**

[]{#vocab.terralid.org#c_7250b71d}

###  Producer
- **Child of**:
 [`contributorType`](#vocab.terralid.org#c_e4ed600b)
- Typically, a person or organisation responsible for the artistry and
form of a media product.
- **Concept URI:** http://vocab.terralid.org#c_7250b71d
- **Other Properties:**

[]{#vocab.terralid.org#c_041e374c}

###  HostingInstitution
- **Child of**:
 [`contributorType`](#vocab.terralid.org#c_e4ed600b)
- Typically, the organisation allowing the resource to be available on
the internet through the provision of its hardware/software/operating
support.
- **Concept URI:** http://vocab.terralid.org#c_041e374c
- **Other Properties:**

[]{#vocab.terralid.org#c_36d34fdb}

###  Editor
- **Child of**:
 [`contributorType`](#vocab.terralid.org#c_e4ed600b)
- A person who oversees the details related to the publication format
of the resource.
- **Concept URI:** http://vocab.terralid.org#c_36d34fdb
- **Other Properties:**

[]{#vocab.terralid.org#c_62875695}

###  Distributor
- **Child of**:
 [`contributorType`](#vocab.terralid.org#c_e4ed600b)
- Institution tasked with responsibility to generate/disseminate
copies of the resource in either electronic or print form.
- **Concept URI:** http://vocab.terralid.org#c_62875695
- **Other Properties:**

[]{#vocab.terralid.org#c_8034b473}

###  DataManager
- **Child of**:
 [`contributorType`](#vocab.terralid.org#c_e4ed600b)
- Person (or organisation with a staff of data managers, such as a
data centre) responsible for maintaining the finished resource.
- **Concept URI:** http://vocab.terralid.org#c_8034b473
- **Other Properties:**

[]{#vocab.terralid.org#c_eed9b69e}

###  DataCurator
- **Child of**:
 [`contributorType`](#vocab.terralid.org#c_e4ed600b)
- Person tasked with reviewing, enhancing, cleaning, or standardizing
metadata and the associated data submitted for storage, use, and
maintenance within a data centre or repository.
- **Concept URI:** http://vocab.terralid.org#c_eed9b69e
- **Other Properties:**

[]{#vocab.terralid.org#c_f5974a2b}

###  DataCollector
- **Child of**:
 [`contributorType`](#vocab.terralid.org#c_e4ed600b)
- Person/institution responsible for finding or gathering/collecting
data under the guidelines of the author(s) or Principal Investigator
(PI).
- **Concept URI:** http://vocab.terralid.org#c_f5974a2b
- **Other Properties:**

[]{#vocab.terralid.org#c_34e78a4f}

###  ContactPerson
- **Child of**:
 [`contributorType`](#vocab.terralid.org#c_e4ed600b)
- Person with knowledge of how to access, troubleshoot, or otherwise
field issues related to the resource.
- **Concept URI:** http://vocab.terralid.org#c_34e78a4f
- **Other Properties:**

 [`tipos de épocas`](#000005)
- Whether a date refers to an archaeological or geological event or
period.
- **Concept URI:** http://vocab.terralid.org#c_5592fbc1
- **Other Properties:**

[]{#vocab.terralid.org#c_ee0cd6f1}

###  archaeological
- **Child of**:
 [`date type`](#vocab.terralid.org#c_5592fbc1)
- Adjective of "archaeology", which refers to the study of the human
past.
- **Concept URI:** http://vocab.terralid.org#c_ee0cd6f1
- **Other Properties:**

[]{#vocab.terralid.org#c_de82ab14}

- **Child of**:
 [`date type`](#vocab.terralid.org#c_5592fbc1)
- Adjective of "geology", which refers to the study of the Earth's
