processes.
- **Concept URI:** http://vocab.terralid.org#c_de82ab14
- **Other Properties:**


[]{#vocab.terralid.org#c_458c8d5b}

##  dating technique
- **Child of**:
 [`méthodes`](#000023)
- Determining the date of an event or in time or relatively to other
other events.
- **Concept URI:** http://vocab.terralid.org#c_458c8d5b
- **Other Properties:**

[]{#vocab.terralid.org#c_70421df0}

###  absolute dating
- **Child of**:
 [`dating technique`](#vocab.terralid.org#c_458c8d5b)
- Dating technique that results dates in calendar years.
- **Concept URI:** http://vocab.terralid.org#c_70421df0
- **Other Properties:**

[]{#vocab.terralid.org#c_bf02bc84}

####  dendrochronology
- **Child of**:
 [`absolute dating`](#vocab.terralid.org#c_70421df0)
- The measurement of a sequence of tree growth rings and their
relative differences in width and the comparison of this information
to known reference curves.
- **Concept URI:** http://vocab.terralid.org#c_bf02bc84
- **Other Properties:**

[]{#vocab.terralid.org#c_e71424b2}

####  lichenometry
- **Child of**:
 [`absolute dating`](#vocab.terralid.org#c_70421df0)
- The measurement of lichens' size on rock surfaces.
- **Concept URI:** http://vocab.terralid.org#c_e71424b2
- **Other Properties:**

[]{#vocab.terralid.org#c_14f0b363}

####  luminescence dating
- **Child of**:
 [`absolute dating`](#vocab.terralid.org#c_70421df0)
- The measurement of the released stored energy in a material in the
form of light to determine the material's age.
- **Concept URI:** http://vocab.terralid.org#c_14f0b363
- **Other Properties:**

[]{#vocab.terralid.org#c_8d044281}

#####  optically stimulated luminescence dating
- **Child of**:
 [`luminescence dating`](#vocab.terralid.org#c_14f0b363)
- The measurement of the stored energy in a material in the form of
light released by exposing the material to a strong light source (e.g.
lamp or laser) to determine the material's age.
- **Alternate labels:**
OSL
- **Concept URI:** http://vocab.terralid.org#c_8d044281
- **Other Properties:**

[]{#vocab.terralid.org#c_8650222c}

#####  thermoluminescence dating
- **Child of**:
 [`luminescence dating`](#vocab.terralid.org#c_14f0b363)
- The measurement of the stored energy in a material in the form of
light released by heating the material to determine the material's
age.
- **Alternate labels:**
TL
- **Concept URI:** http://vocab.terralid.org#c_8650222c
- **Other Properties:**

[]{#vocab.terralid.org#c_3f00a8dc}

####  radiometric dating
- **Child of**:
 [`absolute dating`](#vocab.terralid.org#c_70421df0)
- A family of dating methods that rely on the decay of radioactive
isotopes.
- **Alternate labels:**
radioactive dating
- **Concept URI:** http://vocab.terralid.org#c_3f00a8dc
- **Other Properties:**

[]{#vocab.terralid.org#c_44573a53}

#####  fission track dating
- **Child of**:
 [`radiometric dating`](#vocab.terralid.org#c_3f00a8dc)
- Determining the age of a glassy material by counting the number of
damage trails created through the radioactive decay of uranium atoms
in the material.
- **Concept URI:** http://vocab.terralid.org#c_44573a53
- **Other Properties:**

[]{#vocab.terralid.org#c_ade3d830}

#####  potassium-argon dating
- **Child of**:
 [`radiometric dating`](#vocab.terralid.org#c_3f00a8dc)
- Determination of the age through the ratio of a radioactive
potassium isotope and the argon isotope it decays into.
- **Alternate labels:**
K-Ar dating
- **Concept URI:** http://vocab.terralid.org#c_ade3d830
- **Other Properties:**

[]{#vocab.terralid.org#c_025785fd}

######  argon-argon dating
- **Child of**:
 [`potassium-argon dating`](#vocab.terralid.org#c_ade3d830)
- A dating method superseding potassium-argon dating by allowing age
determination from a single analysis. This is achieved by converting a
controlled amount of the radioactive potassium isotope to a not
naturally occurring argon isotope by neutron irradiation.
- **Alternate labels:**
Ar-Ar dating
- **Concept URI:** http://vocab.terralid.org#c_025785fd
- **Other Properties:**

[]{#vocab.terralid.org#c_f0e6044b}

#####  uranium-series dating
- **Child of**:
 [`radiometric dating`](#vocab.terralid.org#c_3f00a8dc)
- Determination of the age by determining the extent to which the
secular equilibrium between 230Th and 234U was restored in a material
that was nominally thorium-free at the point of its creation.
- **Alternate labels:**
U-series dating
- **Concept URI:** http://vocab.terralid.org#c_f0e6044b
- **Other Properties:**

[]{#vocab.terralid.org#c_4b83987e}

#####  radiocarbon dating
- **Child of**:
 [`radiometric dating`](#vocab.terralid.org#c_3f00a8dc)
- Determination of the age through the remaining radioactivity of
carbon 14 in biogenic materials.
- **Concept URI:** http://vocab.terralid.org#c_4b83987e
- **Other Properties:**

[]{#vocab.terralid.org#c_acb27762}

#####  uranium-lead dating
- **Child of**:
 [`radiometric dating`](#vocab.terralid.org#c_3f00a8dc)
- Determination of the age through the two decay series of uranium
isotopes to lead isotopes.
- **Alternate labels:**
U-Pb dating
- **Concept URI:** http://vocab.terralid.org#c_acb27762
- **Other Properties:**

[]{#vocab.terralid.org#c_f21ed82d}

#####  rubidium-strontium dating
- **Child of**:
 [`radiometric dating`](#vocab.terralid.org#c_3f00a8dc)
- Determination of the age through the ratio of a radioactive rubidium
isotope and the strontium isotope it decays into.
- **Alternate labels:**
Rb-Sr dating
- **Concept URI:** http://vocab.terralid.org#c_f21ed82d
- **Other Properties:**

[]{#vocab.terralid.org#c_5dec7fcf}

#####  lutetium-hafnium dating
- **Child of**:
 [`radiometric dating`](#vocab.terralid.org#c_3f00a8dc)
- Determination of the age through the ratio of a radioactive lutetium
isotope and the hafnium isotope it decays into.
- **Alternate labels:**
Lu-Hf dating
- **Concept URI:** http://vocab.terralid.org#c_5dec7fcf
- **Other Properties:**

[]{#vocab.terralid.org#c_c8c1af39}

#####  samarium-neodymium dating
- **Child of**:
 [`radiometric dating`](#vocab.terralid.org#c_3f00a8dc)
- Determination of the age through the ratio of a radioactive samarium
isotope and the neodymium isotope it decays into.
- **Alternate labels:**
Sm-Nd dating
- **Concept URI:** http://vocab.terralid.org#c_c8c1af39
- **Other Properties:**

[]{#vocab.terralid.org#c_eb290340}

#####  rhenium-osmium dating
- **Child of**:
 [`radiometric dating`](#vocab.terralid.org#c_3f00a8dc)
- Determination of the age through the ratio of a radioactive rhenium
isotope and the osmium isotope it decays into.
- **Alternate labels:**
Rh-Os dating
- **Concept URI:** http://vocab.terralid.org#c_eb290340
- **Other Properties:**

[]{#vocab.terralid.org#c_f98943fa}

#####  lead-lead dating
- **Child of**:
 [`radiometric dating`](#vocab.terralid.org#c_3f00a8dc)
- Determination of the age through the ratios of the the lead isotopes
resulting from the radioactive decay of uranium and the stable lead
isotope ratio.
- **Alternate labels:**
Pb-Pb dating
- **Concept URI:** http://vocab.terralid.org#c_f98943fa
- **Other Properties:**

[]{#vocab.terralid.org#c_e6861de4}

####  rehydroxylation dating
- **Child of**:
 [`absolute dating`](#vocab.terralid.org#c_70421df0)
- The measurement of the hydroxyl content in fired clay and the
determination of the related chemical kinetics to reconstruct the date
of the material.
- **Alternate labels:**
RHX
- **Concept URI:** http://vocab.terralid.org#c_e6861de4
- **Other Properties:**

[]{#vocab.terralid.org#c_25bf9b34}

###  relative dating
- **Child of**:
 [`dating technique`](#vocab.terralid.org#c_458c8d5b)
- Dating technique that allows to put objects in a sequence relative
to each other that cannot be tied to calendar years.
- **Concept URI:** http://vocab.terralid.org#c_25bf9b34
- **Other Properties:**

[]{#vocab.terralid.org#c_e2a8665e}

####  seriation
- **Child of**:
 [`relative dating`](#vocab.terralid.org#c_25bf9b34)
- Recording the stylistic details of a group of items and ordering
them in a sequence based defined typological features.
- **Concept URI:** http://vocab.terralid.org#c_e2a8665e
- **Other Properties:**

[]{#vocab.terralid.org#c_e1bf335f}

####  archaeomagnetic dating
- **Child of**:
 [`relative dating`](#vocab.terralid.org#c_25bf9b34)
- The measurement of the direction and intensity of the magnetic
information in materials and its comparison to known records of
fluctuations in the magnetic field of the Earth. Archaeomagnetic
dating refers to the dating of anthropogenic materials (e.g, fired
clay), while palaeomagnetic dating refers to the dating of geogenic
origin (e.g. rocks).
- **Alternate labels:**
palaeomagnetic dating
- **Concept URI:** http://vocab.terralid.org#c_e1bf335f
- **Other Properties:**

[]{#vocab.terralid.org#c_b767c559}

####  biostratigraphy
- **Child of**:
 [`relative dating`](#vocab.terralid.org#c_25bf9b34)
- The determination of the relative sequence of sediments or rocks
based on the fossils found in them.
- **Concept URI:** http://vocab.terralid.org#c_b767c559
- **Other Properties:**

[]{#vocab.terralid.org#c_5ef3d79f}

####  tephrochronology
- **Child of**:
 [`relative dating`](#vocab.terralid.org#c_25bf9b34)
- The determination of the relative sequence of volcanic deposits
(tephra), especially through studying their stratigraphy (tephra).
- **Concept URI:** http://vocab.terralid.org#c_5ef3d79f
- **Other Properties:**


[]{#vocab.terralid.org#c_6ae288a3}

##  analytical uncertainty
- **Child of**:
 [`Konzepte`](#000024)
- An expression for the statistical dispersion of a measured value.
- **Alternate labels:**
measurement uncertainty
- **Concept URI:** http://vocab.terralid.org#c_6ae288a3
- **Other Properties:**

[]{#vocab.terralid.org#c_1c8f3d46}

###  standard error
- **Child of**:
 [`analytical uncertainty`](#vocab.terralid.org#c_6ae288a3)
- The standard error of the mean (SEM) is data item denoting the
standard deviation of the sample-mean's estimate of a population mean.
It is calculated by dividing the sample standard deviation (i.e., the
sample-based estimate of the standard deviation of the population) by
the square root of n , the size (number of observations) of the
sample. A measure of dispersion applied to means across hypothetical
repeated random samples.
- **Alternate labels:**
SE
- **Concept URI:** http://vocab.terralid.org#c_1c8f3d46
- **Other Properties:**

[]{#vocab.terralid.org#c_ed11712a}

###  standard deviation
- **Child of**:
 [`analytical uncertainty`](#vocab.terralid.org#c_6ae288a3)
- The standard deviation of a random variable, statistical population,
data set, or probability distribution is a measure of variation which
correspond to the average distance from the mean of the data set to
any given point of that dataset. It also corresponds to the square
root of its variance.
- **Alternate labels:**
SD
- **Concept URI:** http://vocab.terralid.org#c_ed11712a
- **Other Properties:**


[]{#vocab.terralid.org#c_c8cb9106}

##  identifier
- **Child of**:
 [`objetos proposicionais`](#000022)
- A string unique for a specific resource or feature.
- **Concept URI:** http://vocab.terralid.org#c_c8cb9106
- **Other Properties:**

[]{#vocab.terralid.org#c_f260a576}

###  w3id
- **Child of**:
 [`identifier`](#vocab.terralid.org#c_c8cb9106)
- Mostly used to publish vocabularies and ontologies. The letters ‘w3’
stand for “World Wide Web”.
- **Alternate labels:**
Permanent identifier for Web applications
- **Concept URI:** http://vocab.terralid.org#c_f260a576
- **Other Properties:**

[]{#vocab.terralid.org#c_efbf8c56}

###  URN
- **Child of**:
 [`identifier`](#vocab.terralid.org#c_c8cb9106)
- A unique and persistent identifier of an electronic document. The
syntax is: urn:<NID>:<NSS>. The leading urn: sequence is case-
insensitive, <NID> is the namespace identifier, <NSS> is the
namespace-specific string.
- **Alternate labels:**
Uniform Resource Name
- **Concept URI:** http://vocab.terralid.org#c_efbf8c56
- **Other Properties:**

[]{#vocab.terralid.org#c_cca94dd2}

###  URL
- **Child of**:
 [`identifier`](#vocab.terralid.org#c_c8cb9106)
- Also known as web address, a URL is a specific character string that
constitutes a reference to a resource. The syntax is:
scheme://domain:port/path?query_string#fragment_id.
- **Alternate labels:**
Uniform Resource Locator
- **Concept URI:** http://vocab.terralid.org#c_cca94dd2
- **Other Properties:**

[]{#vocab.terralid.org#c_49ebd1fc}

###  UPC
- **Child of**:
 [`identifier`](#vocab.terralid.org#c_c8cb9106)
- A barcode symbology used for tracking trade items in stores. Its
most common form, the UPC-A, consists of 12 numerical digits.
- **Alternate labels:**
Universal Product Code
- **Concept URI:** http://vocab.terralid.org#c_49ebd1fc
- **Other Properties:**

[]{#vocab.terralid.org#c_b573f515}

###  SWHID
- **Child of**:
 [`identifier`](#vocab.terralid.org#c_c8cb9106)
- A unique and persistent identifier for software code artifacts
commonly found in version control systems such as code files and
commits available in the Software Heritage Archive. It consists of the
mandatory identifier of the record in the Software Heritage archive
and an optional list of qualifiers to provide information about the
object’s context.
- **Alternate labels:**
SoftWare Hash Identifier 
- **Concept URI:** http://vocab.terralid.org#c_b573f515
- **Other Properties:**

[]{#vocab.terralid.org#c_96a8c2b8}

###  RRID
- **Child of**:
 [`identifier`](#vocab.terralid.org#c_c8cb9106)
- A character string used to uniquely identify key inputs to an
experiment including the so-called “key biological resources” as
defined by the National Institutes of Health, and related tools such
as core facilities and databases. An RRID name is divided into two
parts, the authority and a local identifier, separated by an
underscore.
- **Alternate labels:**
Research Resource IDentifier
- **Concept URI:** http://vocab.terralid.org#c_96a8c2b8
- **Other Properties:**

[]{#vocab.terralid.org#c_b10276d1}

###  RAiD
- **Child of**:
 [`identifier`](#vocab.terralid.org#c_c8cb9106)
- A unique and persistent identifier for research projects.
- **Alternate labels:**
Research Activity Identifier 
- **Concept URI:** http://vocab.terralid.org#c_b10276d1
- **Other Properties:**

[]{#vocab.terralid.org#c_ff6b1f44}

###  PURL
- **Child of**:
 [`identifier`](#vocab.terralid.org#c_c8cb9106)
- A PURL has three parts: (1) a protocol, (2) a resolver address, and
(3) a name.
- **Alternate labels:**
Persistent Uniform Resource Locator
- **Concept URI:** http://vocab.terralid.org#c_ff6b1f44
- **Other Properties:**

[]{#vocab.terralid.org#c_491eec33}

###  PMID
- **Child of**:
 [`identifier`](#vocab.terralid.org#c_c8cb9106)
- A unique number assigned to each PubMed record.
- **Alternate labels:**
PubMed identifier
- **Concept URI:** http://vocab.terralid.org#c_491eec33
- **Other Properties:**

[]{#vocab.terralid.org#c_19665f86}

###  LSID
- **Child of**:
 [`identifier`](#vocab.terralid.org#c_c8cb9106)
- A unique identifier for data in the Life Science domain. Format:
urn:lsid:authority:namespace:identifier:revision.
- **Alternate labels:**
Life Science Identifiers
- **Concept URI:** http://vocab.terralid.org#c_19665f86
- **Other Properties:**

[]{#vocab.terralid.org#c_036b6cbe}

###  LISSN
- **Child of**:
 [`identifier`](#vocab.terralid.org#c_c8cb9106)
- **Alternate labels:**
Linking ISSN
- **Concept URI:** http://vocab.terralid.org#c_036b6cbe
- **Other Properties:**

[]{#vocab.terralid.org#c_a61ff4a5}

###  ISTC
- **Child of**:
 [`identifier`](#vocab.terralid.org#c_c8cb9106)
- A unique “number” assigned to a textual work. An ISTC consists of 16
numbers and/or letters.
- **Alternate labels:**
International Standard Text Code
- **Concept URI:** http://vocab.terralid.org#c_a61ff4a5
- **Other Properties:**

[]{#vocab.terralid.org#c_23aeb12c}

###  ISSN
- **Child of**:
 [`identifier`](#vocab.terralid.org#c_c8cb9106)
- A unique 8-digit number used to identify a print or electronic
periodical publication.
- **Alternate labels:**
International Standard Serial Number
- **Concept URI:** http://vocab.terralid.org#c_23aeb12c
- **Other Properties:**

[]{#vocab.terralid.org#c_664844dc}

###  ISBN
- **Child of**:
 [`identifier`](#vocab.terralid.org#c_c8cb9106)
- A unique numeric book identifier. There are 2 formats: a 10-digit
ISBN format and a 13-digit ISBN.
- **Alternate labels:**
International Standard Book Number
- **Concept URI:** http://vocab.terralid.org#c_664844dc
- **Other Properties:**

[]{#vocab.terralid.org#c_bb41a882}

###  IGSN
- **Child of**:
 [`identifier`](#vocab.terralid.org#c_c8cb9106)
- A code that uniquely identifies samples from our natural environment
and related features-of-interest.
- **Alternate labels:**
International Generic Sample Number
- **Concept URI:** http://vocab.terralid.org#c_bb41a882
- **Other Properties:**

[]{#vocab.terralid.org#c_db36371f}

###  Handle
- **Child of**:
 [`identifier`](#vocab.terralid.org#c_c8cb9106)
- This refers specifically to an ID in the Handle system operated by
the Corporation for National Research Initiatives (CNRI).
- **Concept URI:** http://vocab.terralid.org#c_db36371f
- **Other Properties:**

[]{#vocab.terralid.org#c_9cdedeb0}

###  EISSN
- **Child of**:
 [`identifier`](#vocab.terralid.org#c_c8cb9106)
- ISSN used to identify periodicals in electronic form (eISSN or
e-ISSN).
- **Alternate labels:**
Electronic International Standard Serial Number
- **Concept URI:** http://vocab.terralid.org#c_9cdedeb0
- **Other Properties:**

[]{#vocab.terralid.org#c_91caa317}

###  EAN13
- **Child of**:
 [`identifier`](#vocab.terralid.org#c_c8cb9106)
- A 13-digit barcoding standard that is a superset of the original
12-digit Universal Product Code (UPC) system.
- **Alternate labels:**
European Article Number, 
International Article Number, 
- **Concept URI:** http://vocab.terralid.org#c_91caa317
- **Other Properties:**

[]{#vocab.terralid.org#c_cc3c0083}

###  DOI
- **Child of**:
 [`identifier`](#vocab.terralid.org#c_c8cb9106)
- A character string used to uniquely identify an object. A DOI name
is divided into two parts, a prefix and a suffix, separated by a
slash.
- **Alternate labels:**
Digital Object Identifier
- **Concept URI:** http://vocab.terralid.org#c_cc3c0083
- **Other Properties:**

[]{#vocab.terralid.org#c_e3b9ba37}

###  CSTR
- **Child of**:
 [`identifier`](#vocab.terralid.org#c_c8cb9106)
- CSTR is an identifier based on the Chinese National Standard GB/T
32843—2016 “Science and technology resource identification”, providing
a unique identification service for scientific data, papers,
scientific institutions, researchers, scientific instruments, patents
and other scientific and technological resources.
- **Alternate labels:**
Common Science and Technology Resources Identifier
- **Concept URI:** http://vocab.terralid.org#c_e3b9ba37
- **Other Properties:**

[]{#vocab.terralid.org#c_58ef12c9}

###  bibcode
- **Child of**:
 [`identifier`](#vocab.terralid.org#c_c8cb9106)
- A standardized 19-character identifier according to the syntax
yyyyjjjjjvvvvmppppa. See http://info-uri.info/registry/OAIHandler?verb
=GetRecord&metadataPrefix=reg&identifier=info:bibcode/.
- **Alternate labels:**
Astrophysics Data System bibliographic codes
- **Concept URI:** http://vocab.terralid.org#c_58ef12c9
- **Other Properties:**

[]{#vocab.terralid.org#c_14d152fa}

###  ArXiv
- **Child of**:
 [`identifier`](#vocab.terralid.org#c_c8cb9106)
- arXiv.org is a repository of preprints of scientific papers in the
fields of mathematics, physics, astronomy, computer science,
quantitative biology, statistics, and quantitative finance.
- **Alternate labels:**
arXiv identifier
- **Concept URI:** http://vocab.terralid.org#c_14d152fa
- **Other Properties:**

[]{#vocab.terralid.org#c_9c7b1aa6}

###  ARK
- **Child of**:
 [`identifier`](#vocab.terralid.org#c_c8cb9106)
- A URI designed to support long-term access to information objects.
In general, ARK syntax is of the form (brackets, []. indicate optional
elements): [http://NMA/]ark:/NAAN/Name [Qualifier].
- **Alternate labels:**
Archival Resource Key
- **Concept URI:** http://vocab.terralid.org#c_9c7b1aa6
- **Other Properties:**


[]{#vocab.terralid.org#c_2c9da0f0}

 [`Konzepte`](#000024)
- Describes a relationship between the source being regstered (A) and
the related source (B).

- **Other Properties:**

 [`relationType`](#vocab.terralid.org#c_2c9da0f0)
- indicates A is a translation of B

[]{#vocab.terralid.org#c_50f18eec}

 [`relationType`](#vocab.terralid.org#c_2c9da0f0)
- indicates A collects B

- **Other Properties:**

[]{#vocab.terralid.org#c_7209d768}

###  IsCollectedBy
- **Child of**:
 [`relationType`](#vocab.terralid.org#c_2c9da0f0)
- indicates A is collected by B

###  IsObsoletedBy

- **Child of**:
 [`relationType`](#vocab.terralid.org#c_2c9da0f0)
- indicates A replaces B

[]{#vocab.terralid.org#c_83af8ee5}

###  Requires
- **Child of**:
 [`relationType`](#vocab.terralid.org#c_2c9da0f0)
- indicates A requires B

[]{#vocab.terralid.org#c_f0352501}

###  IsRequiredBy
- **Child of**:
 [`relationType`](#vocab.terralid.org#c_2c9da0f0)
- indicates A is required by B

- **Other Properties:**

[]{#vocab.terralid.org#c_d7654681}

###  IsSourceOf
- **Child of**:
 [`relationType`](#vocab.terralid.org#c_2c9da0f0)
- indicates A is a source upon which B is based

[]{#vocab.terralid.org#c_8aa44c44}

- **Child of**:
 [`relationType`](#vocab.terralid.org#c_2c9da0f0)
- indicates B is a source upon which A is based
- **Concept URI:** http://vocab.terralid.org#c_8aa44c44
- **Other Properties:**

[]{#vocab.terralid.org#c_1565380d}

###  Reviews
- **Child of**:
 [`relationType`](#vocab.terralid.org#c_2c9da0f0)
- indicates that A is a review of B
- **Concept URI:** http://vocab.terralid.org#c_1565380d
- **Other Properties:**

[]{#vocab.terralid.org#c_8941e0d4}

###  IsReviewedBy
- **Child of**:
 [`relationType`](#vocab.terralid.org#c_2c9da0f0)
- indicates that A is reviewed by B
- **Concept URI:** http://vocab.terralid.org#c_8941e0d4
- **Other Properties:**

[]{#vocab.terralid.org#c_58ed789b}

###  IsIdenticalTo
- **Child of**:
 [`relationType`](#vocab.terralid.org#c_2c9da0f0)
- indicates that A is identical to B, for use when there is a need to
register two separate instances of the same resource
- **Concept URI:** http://vocab.terralid.org#c_58ed789b
- **Other Properties:**

[]{#vocab.terralid.org#c_f214a28f}

###  IsOriginalFormOf
- **Child of**:
 [`relationType`](#vocab.terralid.org#c_2c9da0f0)
- indicates A is the original form of B

- **Other Properties:**

[]{#vocab.terralid.org#c_4ccf0580}

###  IsVariantFormOf
- **Child of**:
 [`relationType`](#vocab.terralid.org#c_2c9da0f0)
- indicates A is a variant or different form of B
- **Concept URI:** http://vocab.terralid.org#c_4ccf0580
- **Other Properties:**

[]{#vocab.terralid.org#c_ad976a89}

###  Compiles
- **Child of**:
 [`relationType`](#vocab.terralid.org#c_2c9da0f0)
- indicates B is the result of a compile or creation event using A

 [`relationType`](#vocab.terralid.org#c_2c9da0f0)
- indicates B is used to compile or create A
- **Concept URI:** http://vocab.terralid.org#c_38b56cad
- **Other Properties:**

[]{#vocab.terralid.org#c_85ea27dc}

###  Documents
- **Child of**:
 [`relationType`](#vocab.terralid.org#c_2c9da0f0)
- indicates A is documentation about/explaining B
- **Concept URI:** http://vocab.terralid.org#c_85ea27dc
- **Other Properties:**

[]{#vocab.terralid.org#c_d85f7b3a}

###  IsDocumentedBy
- **Child of**:
 [`relationType`](#vocab.terralid.org#c_2c9da0f0)
- indicates B is documentation about/explaining A
- **Concept URI:** http://vocab.terralid.org#c_d85f7b3a
- **Other Properties:**

[]{#vocab.terralid.org#c_9e3bfeb6}

 [`relationType`](#vocab.terralid.org#c_2c9da0f0)

[]{#vocab.terralid.org#c_6510b31c}

 [`relationType`](#vocab.terralid.org#c_2c9da0f0)

[]{#vocab.terralid.org#c_50fb5931}

 [`relationType`](#vocab.terralid.org#c_2c9da0f0)

[]{#vocab.terralid.org#c_99ef8f93}

- **Child of**:
 [`relationType`](#vocab.terralid.org#c_2c9da0f0)

[]{#vocab.terralid.org#c_43052c1f}

###  IsPartOf

 [`relationType`](#vocab.terralid.org#c_2c9da0f0)
- indicates A is a portion of B; may be used for elements of a series

[]{#vocab.terralid.org#c_36fff7dd}

###  IsPreviousVersionOf

 [`relationType`](#vocab.terralid.org#c_2c9da0f0)
- indicates A is a previous edition of B

[]{#vocab.terralid.org#c_5bf18e08}

###  IsNewVersionOf

 [`relationType`](#vocab.terralid.org#c_2c9da0f0)

[]{#vocab.terralid.org#c_d1c5bee2}

###  IsVersionOf
- **Child of**:
 [`relationType`](#vocab.terralid.org#c_2c9da0f0)
- indicates A is a version of B
- **Concept URI:** http://vocab.terralid.org#c_d1c5bee2

[]{#vocab.terralid.org#c_4e153d89}

###  HasVersion

 [`relationType`](#vocab.terralid.org#c_2c9da0f0)
- indicates A has a version B

[]{#vocab.terralid.org#c_b8eafed9}

###  IsMetadataFor

 [`relationType`](#vocab.terralid.org#c_2c9da0f0)

[]{#vocab.terralid.org#c_f14ca7a6}

###  HasMetadata
- **Child of**:
 [`relationType`](#vocab.terralid.org#c_2c9da0f0)
- indicates resource A has additional metadata B
- **Concept URI:** http://vocab.terralid.org#c_f14ca7a6
- **Other Properties:**

[]{#vocab.terralid.org#c_c3c2de24}

###  IsDescribedBy
- **Child of**:
 [`relationType`](#vocab.terralid.org#c_2c9da0f0)
- indicates A is described by B
- **Concept URI:** http://vocab.terralid.org#c_c3c2de24
- **Other Properties:**

[]{#vocab.terralid.org#c_79a5511e}

###  Describes
- **Child of**:
 [`relationType`](#vocab.terralid.org#c_2c9da0f0)
- indicates A describes B
- **Concept URI:** http://vocab.terralid.org#c_79a5511e
- **Other Properties:**

[]{#vocab.terralid.org#c_ec92c2c0}

###  Continues
- **Child of**:
 [`relationType`](#vocab.terralid.org#c_2c9da0f0)
- indicates A is a continuation of the work B

[]{#vocab.terralid.org#c_33f972fe}

Audio recording

###  gazetteer

[]{#vocab.terralid.org#c_321798be}

 [`gazetteer`](#vocab.terralid.org#c_59d96666)

- **Other Properties:**

[]{#vocab.terralid.org#c_2c0d9965}

####  TGN

 [`gazetteer`](#vocab.terralid.org#c_59d96666)
- The Getty Thesaurus of Geographic Names is a structured vocabulary
for places important in arts and architecture.

- **Other Properties:**

[]{#vocab.terralid.org#c_c18450b7}

####  Geonames
- **Child of**:
 [`gazetteer`](#vocab.terralid.org#c_59d96666)
- Geonames is a geographical database with global coverage.

- **Other Properties:**

[]{#vocab.terralid.org#c_81d9c666}

####  Pleiades
- **Child of**:
 [`gazetteer`](#vocab.terralid.org#c_59d96666)
- Pleiades is a community-built gazetteer for archaeological and
historical sites with focus on the ancient Mediterrean and

- **Other Properties:**

[]{#vocab.terralid.org#c_eb965d6a}

####  iDAI.gazetteer
- **Child of**:
 [`gazetteer`](#vocab.terralid.org#c_59d96666)
- The gazetteer of the German Archaeological Institute.

[]{#vocab.terralid.org#c_d012defe}

####  PeriodO

 [`gazetteer`](#vocab.terralid.org#c_59d96666)
- PeriodO is a gazetteer for chronological information of

[]{#vocab.terralid.org#c_c62b90a7}

[]{#vocab.terralid.org#c_8441accd}

##  mineral texture
- **Child of**:
 [`υλικά μορφώματα`](#000019)
- The relationship between the mineral and the matrix and other
minerals, conveying information about the mineral's formation
conditions.
- **Concept URI:** http://vocab.terralid.org#c_8441accd
- **Other Properties:**

[]{#vocab.terralid.org#c_e74b28a0}

###  intergrowth
- **Child of**:
 [`mineral texture`](#vocab.terralid.org#c_8441accd)
- The state of interlocking of different mineral grains due to
simultaneous crystallisation.
- **Concept URI:** http://vocab.terralid.org#c_e74b28a0
- **Other Properties:**

[]{#vocab.terralid.org#c_dd1113d4}

####  ophitic
- **Child of**:
 [`intergrowth`](#vocab.terralid.org#c_e74b28a0)
- Large and often lath-shaped euhedral or subhedral crystals are
partly or fully included in much larger crystals.
- **Concept URI:** http://vocab.terralid.org#c_dd1113d4
- **Other Properties:**

[]{#vocab.terralid.org#c_cab80d83}

####  intersertal
- **Child of**:
 [`intergrowth`](#vocab.terralid.org#c_e74b28a0)
- Filling of interstitial space with a glass or a vitreous phase with
small crystals.
- **Concept URI:** http://vocab.terralid.org#c_cab80d83
- **Other Properties:**

[]{#vocab.terralid.org#c_1a4f781a}

####  interstitial
- **Child of**:
 [`intergrowth`](#vocab.terralid.org#c_e74b28a0)
- The mineral grew in the interstitial space between grains or
structures.
- **Concept URI:** http://vocab.terralid.org#c_1a4f781a
- **Other Properties:**

[]{#vocab.terralid.org#c_3e50acb7}

####  interlocked
- **Child of**:
 [`intergrowth`](#vocab.terralid.org#c_e74b28a0)
- Intimate intergrowth of randomly orientated mineral grains with no
gaps or cement between them.
- **Concept URI:** http://vocab.terralid.org#c_3e50acb7
- **Other Properties:**

[]{#vocab.terralid.org#c_9e0d2c4a}

####  symplectitic
- **Child of**:
 [`intergrowth`](#vocab.terralid.org#c_e74b28a0)
- The fine-grained vermicular ("worm-shaped") intergrowth of two
minerals, often indicating the breakdown of unstable phases.
- **Alternate labels:**
vermicular
- **Concept URI:** http://vocab.terralid.org#c_9e0d2c4a
- **Other Properties:**

[]{#vocab.terralid.org#c_5eaf970c}

#####  myrmekitic
- **Child of**:
 [`symplectitic`](#vocab.terralid.org#c_9e0d2c4a)
- A symplectitic intergrowth of plagioclase and vermicular quartz.
- **Concept URI:** http://vocab.terralid.org#c_5eaf970c
- **Other Properties:**

[]{#vocab.terralid.org#c_6655861f}

####  poikiloblastic
- **Child of**:
 [`intergrowth`](#vocab.terralid.org#c_e74b28a0)
- Small grains of one mineral are randomly scattered without common
orientation in larger grains of another mineral.
- **Alternate labels:**
poikilitic, 
sieve texture, 
- **Concept URI:** http://vocab.terralid.org#c_6655861f
- **Other Properties:**

[]{#vocab.terralid.org#c_aa5d33c3}

####  disseminated
- **Child of**:
 [`intergrowth`](#vocab.terralid.org#c_e74b28a0)
- The mineral is scattered as small grains throughout another mineral
or the matrix.
- **Concept URI:** http://vocab.terralid.org#c_aa5d33c3
- **Other Properties:**

[]{#vocab.terralid.org#c_436d944e}

####  rimmed
- **Child of**:
 [`intergrowth`](#vocab.terralid.org#c_e74b28a0)
- A mineral grew in the rim of another mineral.
- **Concept URI:** http://vocab.terralid.org#c_436d944e
- **Other Properties:**

[]{#vocab.terralid.org#c_e5b6f80a}

####  amoeboid
- **Child of**:
 [`intergrowth`](#vocab.terralid.org#c_e74b28a0)
- A mineral with irregular outlines and anhedral crystals.
- **Concept URI:** http://vocab.terralid.org#c_e5b6f80a
- **Other Properties:**

[]{#vocab.terralid.org#c_04619e43}

####  cellular (intergrowth)
- **Child of**:
 [`intergrowth`](#vocab.terralid.org#c_e74b28a0)
- Growth of minerals in small roughly regular-shaped rounded
aggregates that resemble the shape of biological cells. Or the porous
texture of a rock characterised by abundant openings or cavities,
which may or may not be connected.
- **Alternate labels:**
porous, 
cavernous, 
vesicular, 
- **Concept URI:** http://vocab.terralid.org#c_04619e43
- **Other Properties:**

[]{#vocab.terralid.org#c_95b262b5}

####  atoll-like
- **Child of**:
 [`intergrowth`](#vocab.terralid.org#c_e74b28a0)
- The growth of a ring-shaped mineral around and surrounded by other
minerals.
- **Alternate labels:**
core texture
- **Concept URI:** http://vocab.terralid.org#c_95b262b5
- **Other Properties:**

[]{#vocab.terralid.org#c_a3d375e7}

###  replacement
- **Child of**:
 [`mineral texture`](#vocab.terralid.org#c_8441accd)
- The growth of a new or chemically different mineral as a reaction
product of the already existing mineral.
- **Concept URI:** http://vocab.terralid.org#c_a3d375e7
- **Other Properties:**

[]{#vocab.terralid.org#c_f478f111}

####  filiform
- **Child of**:
 [`replacement`](#vocab.terralid.org#c_a3d375e7)
- An intergrowth of  different minerals in which the larger crystals
have a fairly regular geometric outline and orientation, and resemble
cuneiform writing or have a thread-like appearance.
- **Alternate labels:**
graphic texture
- **Concept URI:** http://vocab.terralid.org#c_f478f111
- **Other Properties:**

[]{#vocab.terralid.org#c_52eaf264}

####  graphic
- **Child of**:
 [`replacement`](#vocab.terralid.org#c_a3d375e7)
- The growth of elongated rounded aggregates of secondary minerals
inside the primary mineral, often as a result of alteration of the
primary mineral.
- **Concept URI:** http://vocab.terralid.org#c_52eaf264
- **Other Properties:**

[]{#vocab.terralid.org#c_8c47dce3}

####  cellular (replacement)
- **Child of**:
 [`replacement`](#vocab.terralid.org#c_a3d375e7)
- The growth of minerals replacing the content of cells in originally
biological matter such as plants in coal.
- **Concept URI:** http://vocab.terralid.org#c_8c47dce3
- **Other Properties:**

[]{#vocab.terralid.org#c_7b190793}

####  island-shaped
- **Child of**:
 [`replacement`](#vocab.terralid.org#c_a3d375e7)
- The original mineral reacted from the outside, leaving an (island-
shaped) unreacted part surrounded by secondary minerals.
- **Concept URI:** http://vocab.terralid.org#c_7b190793
- **Other Properties:**

[]{#vocab.terralid.org#c_d78ab7be}

####  boxwork
- **Child of**:
 [`replacement`](#vocab.terralid.org#c_a3d375e7)
- Growth of secondary minerals in cavities and along fracture or
cleavage planes from which previously present minerals were removed by
e.g. dissolution, creating a network texture resembling stacked boxes
or honey-combs.
- **Concept URI:** http://vocab.terralid.org#c_d78ab7be
- **Other Properties:**

[]{#vocab.terralid.org#c_36ca06ef}

####  skeleton-shaped
- **Child of**:
 [`replacement`](#vocab.terralid.org#c_a3d375e7)
- Relict of a skeletal crystal of one mineral enclosed in another
mineral species.
- **Concept URI:** http://vocab.terralid.org#c_36ca06ef
- **Other Properties:**

[]{#vocab.terralid.org#c_d3b9f898}

####  cusp-and-caries
- **Child of**:
 [`replacement`](#vocab.terralid.org#c_a3d375e7)
- The growth of a secondary mineral from the grain boundary into the
primary minerals, resulting in a grain boundary between both minerals
with many scallop-shaped, concave embayments.
- **Concept URI:** http://vocab.terralid.org#c_d3b9f898
- **Other Properties:**

[]{#vocab.terralid.org#c_bb44a01a}

####  lacuna
- **Child of**:
 [`replacement`](#vocab.terralid.org#c_a3d375e7)
- The dissolution of small areas of a mineral, often at its rim,
resulting in voids.
- **Concept URI:** http://vocab.terralid.org#c_bb44a01a
- **Other Properties:**

[]{#vocab.terralid.org#c_7fe5c1ff}

####  lamellar
- **Child of**:
 [`replacement`](#vocab.terralid.org#c_a3d375e7)
- The oriented growth of a secondary mineral in elongated shapes
within the primary mineral, usually along cleavage planes, crystal
planes, twin boundaries or fractures.
- **Concept URI:** http://vocab.terralid.org#c_7fe5c1ff
- **Other Properties:**

[]{#vocab.terralid.org#c_7d8b3c14}

####  zonal
- **Child of**:
 [`replacement`](#vocab.terralid.org#c_a3d375e7)
- The evenly-spaced growth of a secondary mineral along the
crystallographic orientation of the primary mineral's grain boundary.
- **Concept URI:** http://vocab.terralid.org#c_7d8b3c14
- **Other Properties:**

[]{#vocab.terralid.org#c_dd91efb2}

####  atoll-like (replacement)
- **Child of**:
 [`replacement`](#vocab.terralid.org#c_a3d375e7)
- The replacement of one mineral by another with leaving the outermost
original mineral unaffected, and constituting the "atoll".
- **Concept URI:** http://vocab.terralid.org#c_dd91efb2
- **Other Properties:**

[]{#vocab.terralid.org#c_d375a5c9}

####  cement-shaped
- **Child of**:
 [`replacement`](#vocab.terralid.org#c_a3d375e7)
- The growth of a dense anhedral alteration mineral in the matrix.
- **Concept URI:** http://vocab.terralid.org#c_d375a5c9
- **Other Properties:**

[]{#vocab.terralid.org#c_0571a899}

####  pseudomorph
- **Child of**:
 [`replacement`](#vocab.terralid.org#c_a3d375e7)
- A secondary mineral completely replacing the primary mineral while
retaining the shape of the primary mineral's grains.
- **Concept URI:** http://vocab.terralid.org#c_0571a899
- **Other Properties:**

[]{#vocab.terralid.org#c_88091e76}

###  deformation-related
- **Child of**:
 [`mineral texture`](#vocab.terralid.org#c_8441accd)
- The alteration of minerals through external mechanical stress.
- **Concept URI:** http://vocab.terralid.org#c_88091e76
- **Other Properties:**

[]{#vocab.terralid.org#c_6783bb4e}

####  cataclastic
- **Child of**:
 [`deformation-related`](#vocab.terralid.org#c_88091e76)
- The fracturing of a mineral due to intense mechanical stress.
- **Concept URI:** http://vocab.terralid.org#c_6783bb4e
- **Other Properties:**

[]{#vocab.terralid.org#c_430fd693}

####  brecciated
- **Child of**:
 [`deformation-related`](#vocab.terralid.org#c_88091e76)
- The fragmentation of a mineral resulting in various angular
fragments
- **Concept URI:** http://vocab.terralid.org#c_430fd693
- **Other Properties:**

[]{#vocab.terralid.org#c_03b13243}

####  broken
- **Child of**:
 [`deformation-related`](#vocab.terralid.org#c_88091e76)
- The fragmentation of a mineral.
- **Concept URI:** http://vocab.terralid.org#c_03b13243
- **Other Properties:**

[]{#vocab.terralid.org#c_3136dfed}

####  translation lamellae
- **Child of**:
 [`deformation-related`](#vocab.terralid.org#c_88091e76)
- The internal deformation of a mineral's crystal due to translational
forces, resulting in the re-orientation of some areas in the crystal
along crystal planes unrelated to the crystallographic axes
- **Concept URI:** http://vocab.terralid.org#c_3136dfed
- **Other Properties:**

[]{#vocab.terralid.org#c_7067812d}

####  pressure twins
- **Child of**:
 [`deformation-related`](#vocab.terralid.org#c_88091e76)
- The internal deformation of a mineral's crystal due to translational
forces, resulting in the re-orientation of some areas in the crystal
along crystal planes along its crystallographic axes.
- **Concept URI:** http://vocab.terralid.org#c_7067812d
- **Other Properties:**

[]{#vocab.terralid.org#c_c8069a37}

####  bending
- **Child of**:
 [`deformation-related`](#vocab.terralid.org#c_88091e76)
- The ductile transformation of a mineral into a bent shape (i.e.,
without breaking it).
- **Concept URI:** http://vocab.terralid.org#c_c8069a37
- **Other Properties:**

[]{#vocab.terralid.org#c_ed4b7838}

####  planar alignment
- **Child of**:
 [`deformation-related`](#vocab.terralid.org#c_88091e76)
- The transformation of elongated or layered minerals into a
(sub)parallel orientation.
- **Concept URI:** http://vocab.terralid.org#c_ed4b7838
- **Other Properties:**

###  other

###  absorption
- **Child of**:
 [`production process`](#vocab.terralid.org#c_25f0249a)
- The material is exposed to another material from which a specific
compound or the entire material gets dissolved into the material.
- **Concept URI:** http://vocab.terralid.org#c_31f5fc72
- **Other Properties:**

[]{#vocab.terralid.org#c_f37a4790}

###  weathering
- **Child of**:
 [`production process`](#vocab.terralid.org#c_25f0249a)
- The material was exposed to react with its environment and meteoric
water.
- **Concept URI:** http://vocab.terralid.org#c_f37a4790
- **Other Properties:**

[]{#vocab.terralid.org#c_6237ab6b}

###  acid reaction
- **Child of**:
 [`production process`](#vocab.terralid.org#c_25f0249a)
- The partial or complete dissolution of all parts of a material when
exposed to acids.
- **Concept URI:** http://vocab.terralid.org#c_6237ab6b
- **Other Properties:**

[]{#vocab.terralid.org#c_5916e942}

###  leaching
- **Child of**:
 [`production process`](#vocab.terralid.org#c_25f0249a)
- The dissolution of specific parts of a material by covering or
submerging it in a solution such as an acid, solvent, or water.
- **Concept URI:** http://vocab.terralid.org#c_5916e942
- **Other Properties:**

[]{#vocab.terralid.org#c_a1b2b905}

###  grinding
- **Child of**:
 [`production process`](#vocab.terralid.org#c_25f0249a)
- The material was mechanically reduced to smaller particles by
applying friction.
- **Concept URI:** http://vocab.terralid.org#c_a1b2b905
- **Other Properties:**

[]{#vocab.terralid.org#c_22488ba9}

###  heating
